# Gotchas (living list)

One line per solved problem. Moved out of `CLAUDE.md` on 2026-09-07 so the agent guide stays
short; **append here** when you solve something real. Newest at the bottom of each section.

## Ghidra / symbols
- Ghidra `X86FunctionPurgeAnalyzer` on huge binaries (165k fns) goes log-silent for hours in a
  quadratic progress-reporting loop — it's working, not hung; verify with jstack + CPU sampling,
  never kill (headless saves only after analysis completes).
- PDB names can contain whitespace ("dynamic initializer for 'x'") which Ghidra symbols reject —
  `ApplyNames.java` sanitizes `\s+`→`_` before `setName`.
- Preserve MSVC decorated names on the first PDB/BSim `setName` attempt so `DemangleAll.java` still
  works; only fall back to `SymbolUtilities.replaceInvalidChars(...)` after Ghidra rejects a name.
- RTTI vtable-slot ports beat low-confidence BSim guesses when slot counts align; preserve the
  compare TSV, then use `LabelApplyForce.java` and demangle.
- `analyzeHeadless.bat` script args: cmd.exe splits on `=`, so `name=0xaddr` arrives as TWO args —
  pass alternating `name addr` pairs instead.
- Ghidra DB after RTTI force pass: 49,082 functions, 40,187 named, 8,895 default-named. Bulk RTTI
  port source is `ghidra_out/labels_rtti_port.tsv`; conflict audit is `ghidra_out/rtti_port_compare.tsv`.
- Quest logic is compiled C++ (161-entry name→allocator table @ 0x00CD52D0, no quest VM) — see
  `docs/engine/QUEST_SCRIPTS.md#quest-binding-compiled-c-classes`. Trust FSE ASLR addresses over BSim names when they clash (0x00CB8110 is
  the CScriptBase ctor, not "CHeroMorphDef"; 0x00CBFAB8 is SetScriptActiveStatus).
- Manifest module labels are NOT trustworthy per file: Ghidra propagates one name over every
  byte-identical body (2,977 "CLandscapeBackgroundPatch::vector_deleting_destructor" rows are dtors of
  many classes). Use `tools/decomp_pipeline/label_trust.py` (address-unique `(module, leaf)` pairs)
  before attributing a function to a class.

## Decomp harness (`tools/decomp_pipeline`)
- `verify_and_land.py` decodes `&lt; &gt; &quot; &apos; &amp;` in payloads, rewrites `__thiscall`→
  `__fastcall`, and strips every line containing lowercase `static_assert`. Never write the literal
  `__thiscall` keyword (VC7.1 C4234); use `FABLE_STATIC_ASSERT` from `rebuild_abi.h` for size checks.
- Workflow `args` arrive in the script as a JSON **string**, not a parsed value — guard with
  `const items = typeof args === 'string' ? JSON.parse(args) : args`.
- EMBEDDED JUMP TABLES (switch >~4 dense cases) cannot be verified by `verify_and_land.py` (objdump
  splits the body at `$Lxxx` labels and elides the zeroed table). Use
  `tools/decomp_pipeline/verify_land_jumptable.py <land.json> <oracle.tsv> [--land]` (raw-COFF
  extraction). Proven on `CKeyRedefiner::GetSubTypeForAction` 0x557CA0 and `AreAllowedToCoexist` 0x5578A0.
- Manifest boundary OVER-CAPTURE: an oracle span `[addr, next_manifest_addr)` swallows int3 padding +
  the next function's head when the next fn isn't in the manifest. Fix with
  `tools/decomp_pipeline/trim_overcapture.py 0x<addr>` / `--oracle <tsv>`; then author the single fn.
  A VOID member forwarder gets tail-call-optimised to `jmp` by VC7.1 while retail kept `push;call;ret`
  (same-length DIFFER, not recoverable); value-returning and cdecl-cleanup forwarders recover byte-exact.
  Backlog: `rebuild/backlog/overcapture-recovery-worklist.tsv`.
- Shared engine headers (`rebuild/include/engine/<Class>.h`, generated from the PDB layouts by
  `gen_class_headers.py`) are the required way to model `this`; `retype_landed.py` converts landed files
  and re-proves parity. A `char`-vs-`bool` field type change DOES change bytes when the store comes from
  a differently typed parameter (0088e920 went MATCH→DIFFER) — the parity gate decides, never assume.
- Heredoc-fed Python that contains `\b`/`\w` regexes gets its backslashes eaten twice in this shell and
  writes real backspace bytes into the file. Write regex-bearing source with the Write/Edit tools.
- A file named `CON` (or any reserved device name) at the repo root makes every `git add`/`status`
  hang forever on Windows. Delete with `del "\\?\D:\Documents\FableTLC\CON"`; a killed git leaves a
  stale `.git/index.lock` to remove.

## Build / frontend
- WinLibs mingw64 g++ builds can die at startup with 0xC0000139 (entrypoint not found) from runtime
  DLL mismatches on PATH — link `-static -static-libgcc -static-libstdc++` (done in FableForge).
- Adding a D3D9 frontend texture (`visual_boot_d3d9.cpp`) requires registering it in BOTH the
  `FableInitialiseVisualD3D9` upload chain AND the `VisualRender2DAdapter`
  `RENDER2D_ADAPTER_ATTACH_TEXTURE` chain; miss the attach chain and the quads draw FLAT WHITE.
  Free frontend resource ids: 101-120 taken; 116-119 are WAVE; About=120, spooky=121/122.
- Frontend visual QA runs headlessly: `rebuild/build_bootstrap.ps1 -RetailFrontendBank <frontend.big>`,
  launch the VisualCheckpoint exe, synth-click via SetCursorPos+mouse_event, screenshot with
  Graphics.CopyFromScreen. `$PID` is read-only in PS. Recipe: `docs/pipeline/VISUAL_PARITY_STATUS.md`.
- Retail Fable.exe must be CLOSED while building: WinMain's real single-instance mutex fails the
  `FABLETLC_WINMAIN_BEHAVIOR` fixture (code=2).
- Profile-name font doubling (`AppendProfileNameText`, ENG_ARIAL): fixed by a half-texel UV inset in
  commit d0becd0 (2026-08-10, merged to main 2026-09-07). Re-verify visually after any Render2D change.

## Formats / data (see `docs/formats/`)
- Fable name hash = **crc0** = reflected CRC-32 poly 0xEDB88320, seed 0, NO final inversion
  (`CCharString::ComputeCRC32` 0x00404310). NOT `0xFFFFFFFF-crc32`, NOT zlib. A game.bin def APPEND
  resolves only if the names.bin CRC is crc0 AND the payload's self global-entry-index back-refs are
  retargeted (component sub-defs are SHARED). See `docs/formats/DEF_LOAD_CONTRACT.md`.
- 3DAF anim payload = `u32 decompSize` + ONE raw LZO1X stream; chunks are `[fourcc][u32 size]`, one
  XSEQ per bone track. EgoCore (`C:\Users\Cornelio\Documents\EgoCoreInspect\EgoCore-master`) is the
  whole-format answer key — check it BEFORE byte-RE; do NOT copy its `CDefStringTable::GetCRC` or
  `classIndex=0`. `docs/formats/BIG_ANIM_FORMAT.md` §9, `docs/engine/DEMON_DOOR_FACE.md`.
- Texture payloads: only MIP 0 is chunked-LZO; mips 1..n-1 are RAW. Info+24 (MipSize0) = mip-0 region
  size, 0 = all-raw. DXT3 Info tail is `02 08`. Writer `tools/texture_build.py`; `docs/formats/TEXTURE_WRITER.md`.
- Compiled-mesh `LODSizes[]` are byte SIZES; every retail 1-LOD type-1 entry appends an UNCOUNTED ghost
  LOD; material lists end with a `DegenerateTriangles` sentinel (STATIC meshes only). Skinned weights sum
  exactly 255, max 3 influences. `docs/formats/MESH_COMPOSE.md`.
- Save edits: any SAVED_ENTITIES cell edit must patch the 36-byte cell descriptor AND sectionLen AND
  chunk1_ulen, then re-sign. `tools/save_edit.py`; grammar in `docs/formats/SAVE_ENTITY_GRAPH.md` §9.5.
- Dialogue join: `data\Defs\<bank>snds.bin` = sorted {crc0("SND_"+entryName), soundID}; soundID == .lut
  clip Index == dialogue.big LIPSYNC id. `tools/dialogue_pipeline.py`, `docs/formats/DIALOGUE_PIPELINE.md`.
- Chest facts: `CChestDef::Transfer` is retail 0x004DE204; `OpenerObject` +0x34, `OpenersRequired`
  +0x38; rewards in `CContainerRewardHeroDef::ObjectFamilies` +0x28.
- New-map registration REQUIRES a FinalAlbion_RT.stb common-header chunk (unchecked map lookup in
  OpenRetailStaticMap 0xB41E50 → CTD 0xA2428A). WER Application-log fault offsets +0x400000 = Ghidra VA.
- Region vector is hard-capped at 142 (owner must be ≤141); the ForgeTest crash at 0x7dd1d3 is
  `CEngineLandscapeMap::OpenStaticMap` zero-filling `malloc(field_04)` with no null check.
- Custom terrain textures: foreground triples are GBANK_MAIN_PC entry IDs in textures.big (156
  `UNASSIGNED_*` 512² slots); pipeline `texture_build.py replace` + `forge stb settex`.

## Modding toolchain (lives in FableForge / ForgeFSE now — `docs/modding/README.md`)
- Active build target since 2026-07-18: FableForge (`D:\Code\FableForge`); FQT is a donor, not the target.
- WLD/BWD authoring is `forge wld compile` / `forge world add-level|install-level|attach-map` /
  `forge stb settex`; `forge validate` cross-checks BWD↔WLD↔STB.
- Do not activate a quest during ForgeTest terrain teleports (quest-region abandon modal blocks
  validation). Wasp Menace renders correctly through ForgeFSE, so the base quest API works.
- Mario rig (`work/mario_hero/stage_bindaxis4`): parent-relative ANIM translations stretch the
  segments; mesh-only rest/inverse-bind edits are insufficient. Parked.
- ForgeFSE canonical fork is `D:\Code\ForgeFSE-retail-shadow`; `D:\Code\ForgeFSE` is stale.
- Ubuntu's `7zip` package (dfsg) cannot read RAR5 archives ("Unsupported Method"); use `unrar` (multiverse).
- Ghidra 12.1 release asset is `ghidra_12.1_PUBLIC_20260513.zip`; the GitHub API is blocked from cloud sessions, so probe the download URL directly.
- `LabelApply.java` accepts the whole manifest at once (57k rows, names with `::` and `<>`): 0 failures; it creates functions Ghidra's auto-analysis missed (12,660 on a fresh import).
- A Linux cloud session can author and self-check candidates (g++ syntax, purity, header policy) but cannot grade parity: keep such batches under `rebuild/candidates/manual/batches/` until the VC7.1 box runs `verify_and_land.py`.
- Wine lane: `cl.exe` 13.10.4035 (DDK 3790.1830) runs under wine32 and reproduces catalog MATCH rows; the DDK `<cXXX>` headers have no `namespace std` using-declarations (overlay shims), its CRT libs need `bufferoverflowu.lib` (`export LINK=bufferoverflowu.lib`), and the whole `lib/wnet/i386` tree is too big to sparse-checkout wholesale — name the four `.lib` files.
- `pgrep -f '<pattern>'` inside a bash `until` loop matches the loop's own command line; use `pgrep -x <name>` when waiting for a process to exit.
- VC7.1 codegen rules learned grading the 2026-10-13 batches (122 rows, 118 byte-exact): a bool result written `return cond;` goes through a full register (`xor ecx,ecx ; sete cl ; mov al,cl`), `return cond ? true : false;` gives the retail `sete al`; a member read before a call only stays before the call if it is read into a local first; a `std::vector`/`std::list` element reached through the real iterator OBJECT (`*(begin()+n)`, `*begin()`) is not CSE'd with the size()/empty() loads — model retail containers with the `CRetailVector`/`CRetailList` templates in `rebuild/src/compiled/00/4a/CQuestManager_GetRegisteredQuestFromIndex_004af3c0.cpp` and `.../00/a3/CClassFactory_DeleteAllClasses_00a37130.cpp`; a store that retail re-reads right after writing it means a `long&` parameter of an inlined helper or a const-ref `max` template (Dinkumware `std::max` shape: `left < right ? right : left`, self-store included); `dec eax ; jne` on an argument is a one-case `switch`, not `== 1`; shared epilogues / `and dword ptr [..],0` zero stores / pass-through `push dword ptr [esp+n]` are `#pragma optimize("s",on)` (the lander sweep finds it); `test ah,5 ; jp` is the ordered `<` (`>=` is `test ah,1 ; jne`); a by-value class return with no `add esp,4` after the call is `__stdcall`; callee-saved registers are assigned in REVERSE order of local declaration (declare the local you want in `edi` last); `wchar_t` needs `<stddef.h>` (no `/Zc:wchar_t` in the retail build).
- Wine lane after a container restart: `wineserver -p` (persistent) before grading, or each `cl.exe`/`link.exe` run boots its own server and one row takes ~40 s instead of ~0.5 s (`env.sh` now does it).
- More VC7.1 codegen rules (batch 5, 53/71 landed): a named `float` local for a call result blocks the sibling-call `jmp` (pointer/long locals do not); an 8-byte POD struct returns in `edx:eax`, any user constructor makes it a hidden-pointer return; a `(long, float)` constructor reproduces an x87+register copy; to extract only the plain destructor `??1` from a polymorphic class use a virtual function plus a NON-virtual destructor (a virtual destructor makes the lander grade the deleting `??_G`); `x * 0.5f` (literal) loads `x` first then `fmul [pool]`, `x * g_extern` loads the extern first; `inc dword ptr [mem]` pairs come from an inlined per-object helper; `Helper(a, b)` keeping `this` in ecx is a __thiscall member on `this`; two named locals for `call() + global`; trust `ret N` over the manifest prototype for the parameter count; `#pragma optimize("s",on)` can keep a frame pointer around a tail jump (BirdFlyToFood/BirdGroundWander Initialise).
- A frame-pointer body with `push dword ptr [ebp+8]` and no spills is a size-optimised TU with global optimisation and /Oy both OFF: `#pragma optimize("gsy",off)` then `#pragma optimize("s",on)` at the top of the candidate reproduces it (plain /Od spills, plain "s" tail-jumps).
- Family landing (2026-10-13, scratch `famscan.py` + `gen/fam.py`): grouping the ≤64-byte un-landed bodies by opcode skeleton gives ~4,800 families; one parametrised source per family lands hundreds of byte-identical shapes at once (factories, ctor/dtor shells, Clone, STL node ctors, persist hooks, static initialisers). Rules learned: a by-value enum argument is a plain 4-byte register value (`long`); class names come from `rtti-vtables.tsv` (vtables with no complete-object locator, e.g. 0x012C2F64.., are a no-RTTI module: name them `CVtable_<va>`); a base/member constructor label is trusted only when its (module, name) is unique in the manifest; namespaced RTTI names are used by their leaf in shape templates.
- VC7.1 codegen (family wave): a CCharString temporary passed to a setter must be a named local in its own scope (`{ CCharString name("LIT", -1); Set(name); }`): a bare temporary reuses the constructor's returned `this` in eax where retail re-derives `lea eax,[esp+4]`; `pop ecx` instead of `add esp,4` after a one-argument call is the `#pragma optimize("s",on)` scheduling (the lander sweep finds it, so one template serves both families); a vtable store BEFORE a run of zero stores means the zeroed members live in an embedded member object with an inline constructor (an init list or body assignments sink the vtable store below them); a vtable store AFTER member destructor calls and right before a base-destructor tail call is an inlined empty intermediate-base destructor, and the derived class must be `__declspec(novtable)` (abstract) for its own store to disappear; `xor eax,eax ; cmp ; sete al` is an int/long result (`return a == K;`), a bool result drops the xor; `if (p) delete p;` keeps retail's explicit null test (plain `delete p` emits none); a static std::list initialiser that stores the global LAST builds the node in a local (`node* s = malloc(..); s->next = s; ...; head = s;`), one that re-reads the global between stores assigns `head` first.
- Dynamic initialisers: VC7.1 puts `??__E` initialisers in `.text$yc` and their atexit destructor thunks in `.text$yd`; `verify_and_land.py` now reads every `.text`/`.text$xx` section and its relocations (was `.text` only), so a static-storage object definition (`CCharString g_x("lit", -1);`) lands the retail initializer body. The thunk is the `_$E2` symbol; a thunk that inlines the destructor (Release-shaped) hides the object's address and type.
- Open family DIFFERs (ledgered in `crawl/gen_tried.txt`, see `docs/journal/FINDINGS_LOG.md` 2026-10-13): two-argument ctor storing both members then the vtable (F15, 76 rows: ten source/flag variants all put the vtable store between the member stores); `??_G` with one member destructor then an inlined intermediate-base vtable store (F73, 14 rows); a one-argument forwarder that retail does not tail-call (F148, 9 rows).
- Family waves 5–6 (128-byte scan, `families/fam.py` templates `w*`): a few retail modules (0x00C09000.., CActionParam*) were built with C++ exceptions ON — the fs:[0] frame + `__ehhandler` funclet; `/EHsc` is now in the lander sweep (`EXTRA_FLAGSETS`) and such bodies land as RELOCATION_MATCH[/EHsc] (the funclet is not compared). An EH state 0 with no visible destructor call means a base/member with an EMPTY inline destructor. VC7.1 also emits a NON-virtual `??_G` scalar deleting destructor for a non-polymorphic class with a non-trivial destructor in any TU that `delete`s it (model: the class + `void Destroy(CX* p) { delete p; }`). Loops: a `dest` written through must be a named local for retail's load-before-test (`T* d = dest;`), `last` of a vector must be cached in a local when the loop body calls a virtual (else VC re-reads it); `for (; a != b; ++first, ++d)` increment order = retail `add` order under "s"; a `const T&` default argument materialised right before each call comes from a literal (`Transfer("K", m, 0)`), named locals get hoisted and shared; a member store BEFORE the vtable store in a constructor is an inlined base constructor (`CBase(&m_value)` when it is a pointer to a derived member); `CCountedPointer::Reset` re-reads `m_pObject` for the new block (`new SCountBlock(m_pObject)`), not the argument.
- Adjustor thunks (`sub ecx,N ; jmp CX::f`, `?f@CX@@W3AEXXZ`): VC7.1 emits one only when the override also sits in the primary vtable, i.e. BOTH bases declare the virtual; model `struct A { virtual void f(); char _body[N-4]; }; struct B { virtual void f(); }; struct CX : A, B { virtual void f(); }` plus a `new CX` helper so the vtables (and the thunk) are emitted; the lander picks the 8-byte thunk over the 1-byte empty override because it is the largest symbol carrying the method name.
- A frame-pointer body that ends in `leave` but still uses esi/edi freely is `#pragma optimize("y",off)` + `("s",on)` (not the `gsy` form, which spills); a member sub-object addressed once (`add esi,N`) and then read through (`[esi+4]`) is a named pointer local to it (`CInterfaceMap* map = &m_interfaces;`).
- The lane's DDK `<algorithm>`/`<xutility>` are NOT the retail Dinkumware revision: DDK `std::copy` inlines `_Copy_impl`, retail calls it out of line with two tag arguments. Reconstruct STL front ends by hand (families g46 `_Uninit_copy`, g27 `_Buynode`, g11/g13 iterator `_Dec`/`_Inc`, g26 `find`, g6 `~_Tree` all landed that way); never rely on the DDK STL headers for byte parity.
- EH frames need something to unwind: a stack temporary with an empty inline destructor gets EH state 0 only if the class is polymorphic (the destructor's vtable store is dead and dropped, but the state stays); a plain struct with `~T() {}` builds without any frame.
- Retail STL is a VC6-era Dinkumware: `_Tree::insert` descends on `key < node.key`, checks `begin()`, steps back with the out-of-line `--iterator`, and calls `_Insert(trynode, wherenode, value, 0)` — a NON-NULL first argument (the parent passed twice at begin()) means "link as the left child"; `_Insert` itself links left when wherenode is the head or (unless the 4th argument forces right) a try-node was supplied or `value < wherenode->key`, fixes head->parent/left/right, zeroes the new node's children, calls the `__cdecl` rebalance(node, &root) and bumps the count. Key signedness shows as `setl/jge` (long) vs `setb/jae` (unsigned long). These tree TUs are `optimize("y",off)` + `("s",on)`; VC7.1 re-uses a dead by-reference argument slot for a hidden-result temporary (`lea eax,[ebp+0Ch]`).
- The script module's `CCountedPointer<CX>` factories (`__fastcall`, hidden result in ecx, 557 bodies) are `return CCountedPointer(new CX(owner, thing, arg));` with a three-level constructor chain inlined: the context pointer stored before the base vtable belongs to a root class (its vtable store is dead), the CScriptThing copy follows the base vtable, the derived vtable precedes the owner/argument stores.
- Wave 10 codegen rules: an argument that is a const reference to an EMPTY tag class (`Insert(where, value, SScalarTag(), 1, true)`) is a 1-byte temporary that VC7.1 homes in a dead argument slot (`lea edx,[esp+10h]` / `[ebp+0Bh]`); give the tag a user constructor (`SScalarTag() {}`) or value-initialisation adds a `mov byte ptr [..],0`. `m_position += size` keeps the old value live and emits `lea edx,[eax+4]` where `m_position = newPosition` emits `add eax,4`; a 4-byte intrinsic `memcpy` into a struct-typed local is a `movsd`, into a scalar local a dword move that forwards into the final store. An implicit destructor of a non-polymorphic aggregate must be declared and defined out of line to be the TU's largest symbol (a `p->~T()` helper inlines it and the lander picks the helper); a `delete p` helper is still the way to emit a non-virtual `??_G`. `CPersistTraits<T>::TransferBinaryIn` TUs are frame-pointer + size (`optimize("y",off)` then `("s",on)` in the source; the lander's own sweep does not include `y`).
- Wave 11 codegen rules: an IMPLICIT conversion temporary (`m_name("LIT")` through `CCharString(const char*, long = -1)`) is re-addressed with `lea` at every use, while an explicit `CCharString("LIT", -1)` temporary reuses the constructor's returned pointer (held in a callee-saved register); a node-list teardown that reads the successor from the old register is `victim = n; n = n->next; destroy(victim)`, not `next = n->next; ...; n = next`; `CCountedPointer::operator=` reads the source's object/block into locals before releasing the destination (retail keeps them in edi/ebp across the destroy call); VC6-era `_Tree::_Copy` keeps the running predecessor in the `parent` parameter's own slot (assign to the parameter), and `operator=` captures `head` in a named local only for the Copy call; `and dword ptr [mem],0` zero stores mark a size (`optimize("s")`) TU.
- The retail STL algorithms are SGI (`sort` = `__introsort_loop` + `__final_insertion_sort` with `__lg(n)*2` and threshold 16, `__copy` with `random_access_iterator_tag`/`Distance*` tags, `__copy_backward`), while the containers are VC6-era Dinkumware; the algorithm TUs are compiled with /Gr (every free template function is `__fastcall`: first in ecx, last in edx, the rest on the stack). The reciprocal-multiply `imul` magic gives the element size (`round(2^(32+shift)/magic)`); the by-value comparator is a 4-byte functor.
- Wave 13 codegen rules: a placement copy (`new (d) T(*first)`) of a POD wider than 8 bytes becomes a block copy through esi/edi unless T has an explicit memberwise copy constructor (then it is direct dword moves with one temporary); an element passed BY VALUE to an out-of-line function is built in the argument area (`sub esp,8 ; mov eax,esp ; mov [eax],..`) only when it has a user copy constructor (a POD is pushed dword by dword); SGI `__distance` is `while (first != last) { ++first; ++n; }` (the increment precedes the count); a two-way `switch` on a small value lowers to `dec eax ; dec eax ; je` under `optimize("s")`; `add esp,4` after a call is not an element stride -- filter esp when reading loop increments.
- Wave 14: SGI `__copy_backward` with the two tag arguments (`ret 0Ch`) copies 12-byte PODs as a block through esi/edi -- a plain `unsigned char[12]` body reproduces it (unlike the forward placement copy, which wants a memberwise copy constructor); a `free(victim)` whose argument is popped into ecx (`pop ecx`) marks a size TU; a persist `Transfer` that forms the last member's address with `add esi,N ; push esi` is the natural `&m_last` of the final call.
- Wave 15: a counted pointer whose zero stores precede the `operator new` call is default-constructed and then assigned (`p = new X(...)` through `Reset`), not constructed from the pointer (that form zeroes after the allocation); a by-value class parameter's destructor is inlined once per return path (an early `return` duplicates it, an `if` body does not); an inlined find that passes a key by reference but compares with an immediate takes the key as an enum converting to the reference's type (a conversion temporary) -- binding the reference directly to the variable reloads it after the call, and `const` locals are not folded either; an uninitialised local read on the untaken branch is homed in a dead argument slot and reproduces from the same source; a bool from a virtual call that survives a destructor rides in `bl`; a `CCharString` literal argument that is re-addressed with `lea` after its constructor is an implicit conversion (`Query("LIT")` with a defaulted length), not an explicit temporary.
- Wave 16: a function returning an 8-byte struct through the hidden pointer needs the struct to be non-POD (a user constructor); a plain two-dword POD comes back in edx:eax; a 4-byte class with a user copy constructor passed by value is `push <reg>` (slot reserved) then `mov eax,esp ; mov [eax],value`, and when the caller has just popped only part of a `__cdecl` callee's arguments the leftover slot is reused for the next call's first by-value copy; a scalar deleting destructor is only emitted when the translation unit also constructs the class (the vtable pulls it in); a frame-pointer forwarder's empty tag lives in the high byte of a dead argument slot (`lea edx,[ebp+13h]`, `lea eax,[ebp+0Bh]`).
- Wave 17: STLport `insert_unique` only reproduces with `_M_insert` taking an explicit iterator out-pointer (`SIterator* Insert(SIterator* result, x, y, value, w)`) that VC homes in the dead value slot, `Insert(&j, y, y, value, 0)` in the begin case and the key comparison written inline (a comparator local object adds a dead slot); a copy-down helper has to be written `if (src == srcEnd) return dest;` (early-out) to get `cmp eax,eax ; jne` -- the `if (src != srcEnd) { ... } return dest;` polarity schedules differently; a by-value class temporary returned through the hidden pointer is re-read from its home slot at every use (address-taken aggregate), where a plain pointer local is kept in a register across calls.
- Wave 18: a counted-pointer temporary's release that retail emits as `mov esi,[eax+4] ; mov ecx,eax ; add eax,4 ; dec esi ; mov edx,esi ; test edx,edx ; mov [eax],esi` (`dec [eax] ; cmp [eax],0` under /Os) is a member function on the pointee calling two inline accessors of a ref-count base (`DecRef(); if (GetRefCount() == 0) Destroy();`): the base pointer used twice is materialised, the stored value forwarded; a single `if (--m_count == 0)` folds to `dec [ecx+4] ; jne`. A copy between two different pointer types (`CDefPointer<T>` from `CDefPointer<CDefBase>`) is never elided, so the source temporary's destructor runs (its null store survives when code follows, is elided right before a return). Under `optimize(s)` VC calls small inline operator= / destructors out of line: `__forceinline` them. A 64-bit range check whose two exits must share specific duplicated return tails reproduces through an inline bool predicate. A pointer-to-member-function call is a single `call [this+N]` only when the class is complete and single-inheritance. Nested-scope locals share one frame slot with later temporaries (`push ecx` frames).
- Wave 19: `/Os` TUs are recognisable by unaligned loop heads and `pop ecx` argument cleanup -- add `#pragma optimize("s", on)`. Struct copies: one register per dword = memberwise user copy constructor; three registers (ebx/esi/edi) = POD of dwords; `rep movsd` = byte block; `mov [eax],ecx ; lea esi,[ebx+4] ; lea edi,[eax+4] ; rep movsd` = pair<long, block> (not reproduced). A twice-used `GetPItem()` with the shared false tail at the end is the nested-if form, not `&&`. Dinkumware `upper_bound` reproduces only with `if (!(key < mid->key)) advance; else count = half;`. A by-value class argument built in a reserved slot (`sub esp,8 ; mov eax,esp`) means a user copy constructor; if that constructor copies a float member VC goes through the FPU, so model the member as a dword when retail moves it with `mov`.
- Wave 20: an EH-framed function's unwind funclets (`Unwind@<va>`, catalogued separately) are a slice of its own object: the `$L<n>` contribution after the function holds the funclets in unwind-map order then the `__ehhandler$` thunk, so land the parent and let `families/funclets.py` + `funclet_offset` grade the funclets from a stub that includes the parent (never author a funclet by hand). A float clamp whose retail stores go through `fld [__real@..] ; fstp` with a load/or/store flag update is a nested ternary (`v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v)`), not an if/else chain -- VC folds literal branches into integer immediates.
- Wave 21: a state-0 unwind funclet that calls a destructor on `this` with no `add ecx, N` means the root base owns the vptr at +0 (`__declspec(novtable)` so it stores no vtable of its own); an empty base lands at +4 and its funclet shows `add ecx, 4` while the parent body is byte-identical -- let the funclet decide the model. Under `/Gy` an inline base constructor used by an EH-framed derived constructor is emitted out of line too, with its own `$L` block, so a funclet extractor must try every `$L` contribution, not the first. An assertion `CDebugBreak()` temporary with an empty body still takes a frame slot (retail keeps the dead high byte); a stream built inside an `if` body is a nested scope (its slot overlaps the outer locals). Byte predicates: `mov al ; test al,al ; setne al` is an `unsigned char` result (`bool` adds `xor eax,eax`); `shr al,n ; and al,1` is a one-bit `unsigned char` bitfield; `mov eax,[esp+4] ; push eax ; call operator delete ; pop ecx` is `delete static_cast<char*>(p)`, a `free(p)` call pushes the stack slot directly.
- Wave 22: a `call [eax+N]` on `this` inside a constructor or destructor body cannot be written directly (VC7.1 devirtualises it, pure virtuals become `_purecall`): put the call in an inline non-virtual helper (`__forceinline` when it holds a loop). A destructor body that runs before any vtable store belongs to a `__declspec(novtable)` most-derived class. A member stored before the EH state-0 store in a constructor is a member of the root base (the root's dead vtable store is dropped). A stack object whose destructor is inline and empty still registers an EH state (the funclet calls the out-of-line copy) with nothing on the normal path. `fld [a] ; fmul [b]` is `b * a` (right operand loaded first). After a one-slot `__cdecl` call, `add esp,4` means the result is returned, `pop ecx` means it is dropped. A global of class type initialised from a constant never gets a `$E` initialiser from VC7.1 (static initialisation); such retail `$E` bodies are link-time inlining.
- Wave 23: in a constructor shell, a vtable store before register-zeroed stores and before the argument store is an embedded member with an inline constructor (init-list members sink the vtable store); stores before the derived vtable store are plain initialisers or a polymorphic root base's members (its own vtable store is dead); two argument stores before the vtable are an inlined `__declspec(novtable)` intermediate base. A virtual call on a member object inside a destructor stays virtual, on `this` it needs a `__forceinline` helper. `optimize("gsy",off)` disables inlining; frame-pointer bodies want `optimize("y",off)` + `("s",on)`. Declaring `void operator delete(void*) throw()` removes the EH state of an inline vector release; a member destructor declared `throw()` as the last call of a destructor drops the trailing `-1` state store. A reference parameter makes `static_cast` to the enclosing object null-check free; converting `this` to a base at a non-zero offset inside an inlined destructor keeps the null check. Retail `CArray<T>` is 0x0c bytes and retail `std::vector` 12 bytes (PDB layouts after them sit 4 bytes high). `PerformActionAndWait(&CAction(creature), false)` builds the action as a temporary in the call. `movsx` of a signed byte feeds a long parameter; `setge` into a `short` member is `m = r >= 0`; chained builder calls through eax/ecx are `__fastcall` members returning `this`. A float returned across a lock destructor is parked in a dead argument slot (name the local). An array with a constructor but no destructor is built with an inline counted loop; arrays with destructors go through `__ehvec_ctor`.
- Wave 23b: the "null-object warning" family is `{ CString line; line.Append(file)->Append("(")->AppendNumber(N)->Append(") : ")->Append(text)->Append("\n"); if (g_pfnLogSink) g_pfnLogSink(line.m_pchData); }` in its OWN block followed by an empty-class temporary `CTrace();` (0x00C16750) that lands on a dead frame byte. An inlined position-list `Find` must take the caller's `position` by reference and return it (the found path re-tests the slot); an address-taken local the DDK homes in a dead argument slot gets a frame slot in retail when it is the caller's variable. Read an init-list value through the already-stored member (`m_pWorld->`) when retail re-reads `[esi]`. A container's root/container destructors may be out of line while its constructor is inline. `__declspec(novtable)` intermediate classes keep only the most-derived vtable stores. A virtual call on `this` in a ctor/dtor through a non-virtual inline helper becomes `call [vtable+N]` (known vtable) or a dynamic dispatch (`novtable`). `fild ; test ; jge ; fadd 2^32` is `(float)(unsigned long)x`. Scalars stored after the member constructors are body assignments, not init-list entries. q.py's default leaf is the oracle's last name component: pass `"??1Class"` when a helper symbol contains it.
- Wave 24a: a CCharString temporary handed to a base constructor must come from an inline helper returning it by value (`static inline CCharString AnimationName() { return CCharString("...", -1); }`): a bare temporary argument pushes the trailing constant first and reuses the constructor's eax, a named local in the body builds it after the base call. A member pointer used as `this` of a virtual call is read through an inline accessor (`GetBody()`) to be loaded straight into ecx. A call result that feeds a later call's object expression (`GetWorld()`) is a named local when retail evaluates it before the argument pushes. `fable_string.h` inlines CCharString's copy constructor: model the class locally when retail calls 0x0099EC30. Tree-node value types: an explicit pair-style member-wise copy whose record members are plain PODs (block copies through re-based pointers), never member-wise record copies. Chunk oracle rows from the family scan carry int3 padding: trim before landing.
- Wave 23f: `CProfileLock` (0xc0ffc0/0xc0ffe0) is 8 bytes (section + entry tick) -- a 4-byte model shrinks the frame by a word; `__declspec(novtable)` on both the CUnknown-style base and the pure-interface base is what removes the base-ctor vtable stores; a Win32 import used twice in one body must be `__declspec(dllimport)` to get retail's `mov ebp,[__imp]; call ebp`; the lane (RTM) folds `&local != NULL` inside an inlined callee and rotates top-tested `for(;;){Pop; if (end) break;}` loops where retail does neither -- park, don't fight.
- Wave 23e: a `__stdcall` helper called inside a scoped lock that carries no EH state is `extern "C"` (assumed non-throwing under `/EHsc`); a record whose member has an out-of-line `operator=` is assigned whole (`m_theme = *theme`, the DDK inlines the implicit assignment) while a record with only plain members plus such a member is out-lined (write the memberwise copy); a string temporary chained into `AppendNumber` (`*CString("G").AppendNumber(i)`) reuses the constructor's returned `this`; field stores that precede a derived vptr store come from the base constructor's body; `memset` must be declared through `<string.h>` (a C++-linkage declaration defeats the intrinsic); a lock-scoped function with several early returns whose epilogues are identical is tail-merged by retail but duplicated by the DDK (0x00CA67B0 parked); `new T[n]` with a class `operator new[]` carries a retail zero-count special case (`operator new(4)`) the DDK never emits (0x00C3F800 parked); under `/GS` the lane compiler caches or copies pointer arguments where retail re-reads their slots (0x00CAB160, 0x00C3BB50 parked).
- Wave 23d: `keep = a; if (b < a) keep = b;` is the only form that reproduces retail's `cmp ; jae ; mov` min (the ternary and the Dinkumware helper differ); a chain of three error-code tests whose fall-through case comes first in retail is a `switch`; the DDK out-lines an implicit or explicitly inline `operator=` of a record owning a member with its own assignment (write the memberwise copy in the body; a nested POD member copies as `lea`+four moves); `wchar_t` is a typedef (`unsigned short`) under the era defaults; a `__fastcall` function returning a class by value takes the hidden result in ecx and its first argument in edx, guarding the result's destruction with a flag word; the DDK (QFE 4035) never shares a frame slot between sibling-scope locals where RTM does (0x00C1A870, 0x00C1A110 parked); declaring the loop index before the frame string fixed the esi/edi colouring in 0x00C0AD10.
- Wave 23c: an error hook called through a global pointer with no `add esp,4` after the call is `__stdcall`; `if (p) { delete p; p = 0; }` keeps the clear before the join (a bare `delete p; p = 0;` puts it after); a float member zeroed in an init list is an immediate store while integer zeros share the zero register; a two-field record's stores follow the source statement order; a member pointer read around a store through it is one named local; a float parameter used twice is re-read from its slot; a `__fastcall` member takes its first argument in edx; `q.py` needs the mangled leaf (`"??1Class"`, `"??0Class"`) when a helper or member name contains the oracle's leaf; a behaviour test must initialise every pointer field the body reads.
- Wave 24b (hand chunks 26-27): a frame-pointer size body with `mov eax,ecx` and no spills is `optimize("y",off)` + `("s",on)` (`gsy` spills `this`); `!(a == b)` over a bool-returning inline `&&` chain gives `mov eax,1 ; test al,al ; sete`; the unsigned fields of a lexicographic `<`/`>` merge into one `sbb eax,eax ; neg eax` tail entered by each `jne` (the signed first field keeps its `setl`/`setg`); a CCharString read for BOTH text and length is ONE null test with the empty-string pointer assigned first and `else length = 0` (two accessors test twice; accessors are written null-case-first); a member stored before the derived vtable and before the argument pushes belongs to a NON-polymorphic base (VC lays it out after the vptr); a record zeroed with immediate stores next to `push 0` immediates holds floats (integer zeroes claim a register and turn the pushes into `push eax`); the counted-pointer release lives on the object (`if (--m_refCount == 0) Destroy();`) so the pointer stays in ecx for the virtual call; an inlined by-value `WriteFloat(float)` parameter is homed in the caller's dead argument slot (the lane reproduces this homing); a byte stored before the derived vtable in a 0-code constructor is an inlined `__declspec(novtable)` intermediate base; a conditional flag toggle fed to a setter is the inline ternary argument; the inlined HasTC probe is the raw bitset test in the `if` (`test ch,1`); STLport list insert reads `prev = pos->prev` into a named local before `node->next = pos`. Lane gaps seen: the first Wine process after a container restart spawns `winedevice.exe` services that inherit the compiler's stdout pipe (the grader hangs on a zombie cl.exe) -- run `wine cmd /c exit >/dev/null 2>&1 </dev/null` once before the first grade; the lane narrows `(x >> 8) & 0xFFFFFF01` to `0x00FFFF01`, sinks 1.0f register stores past later zero stores, and keeps a merged null arm inline where retail places it after the following blocks.
- Wave 24c (hand chunk 29): a base called through `lea ecx,[esi+4]` where retail uses `mov ecx,esi` means the model put the vptr in the derived class -- give the base the virtuals; a size-TU bool-looking return that materialises 1 as `xor eax,eax ; inc eax` is a `long` result; a size TU does not inline a counted/def-pointer temporary's functions (write the release out: `if (p) { p->Release(); p = 0; } p = 0;`); a by-value key whose slot address is passed on is `lea eax,[esp+0Ch]`, a reference is `mov eax,[esp+4]`; `cmp value,member` follows the source operand order (`value != member`); a member pointer used as `this` of the next call goes through an inline accessor to land in ecx; a CRGBColour stored B, G, R, A has a constructor with parameters in that order; the 0x00C3xxxx null-object-call family is a free `__stdcall` (`T* t = o->Accepts() ? o : 0; t->Set(x);`, `not eax` for the rejecting twin); a VC6 list `erase` result and `->next` tail-merge into one `mov eax,[eax]` when the iterator is a 4-byte class returned by hidden pointer; a `list` member built with a default allocator tag passes the tag's address from the high byte of a dead argument slot (`lea eax,[ebp+0Fh]`); the NPlayerGui CDrawBase-derived constructors are `/Os` with `optimize("y",off)` only when they keep a frame pointer. Lane gaps seen: `this` kept in ecx across a call (whole-program codegen, 00c20cb0), a bare `fistp` rounding next to a `__ftol2` conversion (an inline-asm helper in the original, 006472b2), a full-eax zero register with `mov al` returns where the lane makes `xor al,al` + `movzx` (00643e09), 8 dead frame bytes (00c437c0), `mov ecx,ebx` scheduled after __ftol2 rather than before the virtual call (00644334/006443ac).
- Wave 24d (hand chunks 28, 31): VC7.1 passes a by-value `__m128i` in xmm0 and `/GS` then builds an aligned cookie frame (`and esp,-16 ; sub esp,10h`); `_mm_set_ps(x,y,z,w)` is four `movss` into the aligned frame + a `movaps` pair; SGI `vector::erase(pos)` homes its const-ref empty tag in `pos`'s dead argument slot and pushes a null `_Distance*`; a CCharString returned by value and chained, or a pointer built from a call result passed with a literal, must be a named local; a tree `Contains` takes its key by value (`lea` of the slot feeds `find`), iterator in a named local, head read after the call, `long` result via `setne`; `inc dword ptr [ecx+4]` AddRef = inline helper, load/inc/store = direct `RefCount = RefCount + 1`; one member zeroed first then the rest = that member alone in the init list; a pointer-to-member call through {function, this-adjust} needs `__multiple_inheritance` on the forward declaration; `Opened = false` in the body vs the init list moves the byte store across a member constructor call; a `ret 4` on a CEngine "static" is a non-static member whose `this` is unused (`ret` = __cdecl static); `fld arg ; fld member ; fucompp ; test ah,44h ; jnp` is `member != arg`; a by-value CCharString argument copied once and destroyed by the callee (`lea ecx,[esp+8] ; call ~`) is an inlined by-value setter; a flag store duplicated into both arms of a float clamp comes after the clamp; a counted-pointer temporary's add-ref and release share one null test with both count stores kept (`mov [esi],ecx ; mov [esi],eax`); a static_cast from a secondary base adds a null test retail lacks (use pointer arithmetic); a `__fastcall` helper taking the key's address in edx and by-value quads copies them through ecx only. Lane gaps: `fld x ; fdivr 1.0` (x first) vs the lane's `fld 1.0 ; fdiv x` (006d63a0), zero-register coalescing across a call (0049ba70), `push eax` before `mov ecx,esi` (00540120), copy-register allocation (007b52b0, 00a31220, 00b25d20), `return false` tail placement after an inlined release (009ace90 = the 0x00CA67B0 gap), and a stack-imbalanced retail body (00c2d260: six pushed __cdecl arguments never popped).
- Wave 24e (hand chunk 32): `delete[]` through the `eh vector destructor iterator` needs `/EHsc` AND an explicit symbol selector (`leaf` = `??1Class`): the TU's `??_G` body is larger and the lander used to pick the largest function whenever the display leaf (`~Class`) never matched a mangled name (`verify_and_land.py` now honours the payload's `leaf`; chunk sources carry `// Flags: /EHsc` into the payload); a select of a vector's data (`count ? first : this`) that feeds a store in each arm must go through ONE inline `GetData()` (a ternary at the join duplicates the store); the hidden return pointer kept in edx across a call is whole-TU register usage (the callee lives in the same TU: parked); the empty allocator's address passed as `lea eax,[esp+8]` (the dead flag slot of a `??_G`) is not reached by value (`mov eax,[esp+8]`), by reference with a named local (own slot) or by value with a user copy ctor (`push ecx`); a dead `lea eax,[eax+edi*8]` of a reference-bound vector element is not reproduced by a pointer, a reference or a double member read.
- Wave 24f (hand chunks 30, 34, 35): a counted member released with `dec [p+10h] ; jne ; call [vt]` is a LOCAL pointer copy (`--m_p->count == 0` re-reads the member after the decrement); an engine-manager destructor with no vtable store of its own is `__declspec(novtable)` (abstract base); in an inlined constructor the words stored BEFORE the vtable are plain init-list members and the words AFTER it are member objects with their own inline constructors (model the latter as a struct with a constructor); an array zeroed with `rep stosd` is `memset` in the body; a counted block whose destroy is `mov ecx,[eax+8] ; call [eax+4]` holds a pointer-to-member at +4 (not a vtable); a trailing store scheduled after a local's destructor call needs the local in an inner scope; a record whose leading words are copied through eax before a `lea ; push` of a member is a copy CONSTRUCTOR (an operator= schedules the lea early); an argument-carrying `novtable` intermediate base owns the words stored before the derived vtable; a `while (!empty())` body that re-reads begin() is the condition on the member with the body through a pointer local; `cmp byte ptr [..],0` flag tests are `unsigned char != 0` under optimize("s"); a missing closing brace (C1075) makes cl.exe under Wine spin forever (`timeout` every grade; `pkill -x`, never `pkill -f cl.exe`); family-scan rows of 28 bytes ending in a `jne` past their end are prefixes of larger functions (skip them).
- Wave 24g (hand chunks 36, 37): a destructor with no vtable store whose "base" destructor runs on `this` AFTER another member's destructor is a non-polymorphic class holding the polymorphic object as its FIRST MEMBER (VC7.1 emits the non-virtual `??_G` on `delete`; `novtable` would suppress the `??_G` entirely); a size-optimised TU emits an empty class destructor out of line unless `__forceinline`; the lander compiles behaviour tests with the lane's cl.exe, so tests must be VC7.1 C++ (no `std::string::clear`); a 12/16-byte member copied word by word through ecx/edx/eax in a constructor is separate scalar members (a struct member copies through `lea`); the range-destroy helper's allocator tag is `const SScalarTag&` (`lea eax,[esp+7]`); an inline class destructor whose TU also emits a `??_G` needs `// Leaf: ??1Class`; a loop-invariant float computed after the loop-entry test is written inside the loop; a VC6 list constructor inlined is `head = 0 ; node = malloc(16) ; node->next = node->prev = node ; head = node`; three stack arguments each read once are three parameters (a cached register means reuse); a global read through an inline member (`g->Next()`) lands it in ecx; a `do/while` chain walk has no entry null test; `__stdcall`-freed resources leave no `add esp,4`; a mixed `add esp,4` / `pop ecx` destructor needs no pragma games; members stored before the derived vtable belong to the (novtable) base constructor; a counted-pointer by-value argument's destructor drops its dead field clears; `ret 8` on a static is `__stdcall`.
- Wave 24h (agent chunk 33): CIVCountedPointer `operator=(0)` is a release plus a second unconditional zero store; `dec [eax] ; mov eax,[esi+8] ; cmp [eax],0` is `--m_pBlock->m_count; if (m_pBlock->m_count == 0)`; a `__stdcall` 0x00C3 body loading its object into ecx with eax/edx argument loads calls thiscall members on it; `delete g` through a class `static void __fastcall operator delete(void*)` gives `mov ecx,[g] ; test ; mov esi,ecx ; call dtor ; mov ecx,esi ; call`; a 64-bit size guard wants the call as the `if` body with `return 0` as the fall-through; `g->bank->Get(x)` needs `bank` in a named local; two inline CArray accessors re-read the first pointer; a user-constructed 8-byte struct copied bitwise returns through integer moves where a user copy constructor goes through the x87; the retail ParticleGroup is 0x14 bytes narrower than the PDB (4-byte STLport lists, a three-pointer CArray).
- Wave 24i (hand chunk 39, agent chunk 38): a constructor whose scalar stores ALL sit after the vtable store initialises member objects with inline constructors (`CIndex() : m_value(-1)`, `CLink() : m_p(0)`); plain initialisers and body assignments are both scheduled before the vtable store. A counter re-read from memory after every access is `volatile`, and a flag byte read after the counter's store must be `volatile` too. A `??_G` with an inlined counted-pointer release needs `__forceinline` on the release AND on the virtual destructor (else the release stays out of line, or the deleting destructor collapses to `call ??1 ; delete`). A callee with no `add esp` after it inside a loop is `__stdcall`. A vtable fetch shared by two if/else arms = both arms reach the object through the same inline accessor chain. A bool member assigned `a && b` is a full-register 0/1 with the byte store tail-duplicated. `fld [esp+4]` before `push esi` and `fld [esp+8]` after it are one float argument. A CRT import used through `call [__imp_x]` must be declared `extern "C" __declspec(dllimport)` without its CRT header. A member array of objects with inline destructors is destroyed last-to-first by an inlined counted loop. Naming a plain-record member in the initialiser list value-initialises it (whole-record zero fill first). Lane gap (two parked): a `lea ecx,[esi+N]` for a member constructor scheduled after a run of register-zero stores in retail, before them in the lane.
- Wave 24j (hand chunks 40, 42; agent chunk 41): a byte cleared BEFORE the derived vtable store while the string members follow it belongs to an intermediate `__declspec(novtable)` base with an inline constructor (a plain initialiser of the derived class lands after the vtable store in that module). `_Destroy_range(first, last, alloc)` called with `lea eax,[esp+N] ; push eax` (N a dead argument slot) takes the allocator as `const&` bound to a default-constructed temporary. A member re-read from memory as `this` in every arm is a POINTER member. Two constructor arguments loaded before `operator new` = an inline helper builds the object. The thing fetched before the argument push = a local; fetched after = the chained call; an object loaded straight into ecx before its argument = an inline accessor. A manager/player/interpolator loaded before the pushes = a local taken first. `if (a == b) { if (f()) return true; } return false;` gives the shared `xor al,al` tail with `test al,al ; je`; the `&&` form gives full-register 0/1; `if (!f()) return false; return true;` gives `setne`. A tail jump with argument-slot reuse reproduces for a void call as the last statement. A dense two-outcome `switch` is the byte-indexed two-entry jump table (the lander now runs `objdump -d -z`: `-d` alone collapses the table's zero runs). `optimize("s")` on a double-narrowing body makes the lane copy the double through `fld st(0)`. `ret` + an edx:eax class return with ecx=this is a static `__fastcall`. The remainder beside a reciprocal-multiply quotient is `t - q*100`. A base-ctor argument re-addressed with `lea` is an implicit conversion. A `__thiscall` destroys its own by-value counted-pointer parameter at its end. A float parked in the dead argument slot across a call is a named float local. - Wave 24k (hand chunks 44, 45, 46; agent chunk 43): the component lookup used as `this` must use the landed OUT-PARAMETER `GetInterface(T** ppOut, long type)` template (0x007504E0 shape): it leaves the component in eax like retail (`mov eax,[eax+4] ; mov ecx,eax`), the pointer-returning `Find()` model loads straight into ecx/edx (15 shapes parked in hand44 on that alone). `HasInterface`/`HasTC` return the masked `unsigned long` word (a bool return narrows to `shr ; and 1`). An inline setter puts its arguments before the pointer fetch; a static member function is `__cdecl` unless written `static __stdcall` (`ret N`); `wchar_t` is `unsigned short` (no builtin). `delete data` on a class with an inline destructor goes through the out-of-line `??_G`: write `data->~T(); operator delete(data);` with `__forceinline` destructors. A destructor that stores the vtable BEFORE releasing a counted pointer keeps the counted pointer as a member object with its own `__forceinline` destructor (24i's rule, destructor side) over a `__declspec(novtable)` base; a destructor with NO vtable store means no `virtual` anywhere in the model. A zero-initialised result local ASSIGNED the call result (`result = Get();` then `return result;`) keeps the zero in edi and still tail-jumps the last call. `je ; call ; add eax,8 ; jmp ; mov eax,8` = a ternary into a local then one add. A 12-byte frame holding a CCharString above an 8-byte parsed item is ONE local (the item owns the string) whatever the declaration order. A vector-first pointer re-read after a division by the element size is `volatile` with the count in a `long` local. A by-value class argument constructed before any scalar load is a named local; a call result computed before the other pushes is a local; a manager chain loaded straight into ecx is an inline CWorld accessor (and tail-jumps a float-returning callee unless the result goes through a named float local); `lea ecx,[eax+N]` for an embedded member as `this` = an inline accessor on the owner (a reference-returning one gives `add ecx,N`); two separate null tests on a string block are two `m_pData == 0 ? default : field` accessors; one virtual call tail-duplicated into both arms after a hoisted load = `flags |= / &= ; Set(flags)` through an inline accessor; the GetInterface template can be emitted out of line in a TU, so pass the plain function name as the leaf. Lane gaps (parked): a by-value 4-byte handle the retail loads straight into edi (the lane loads ecx then copies); a `time < end` compare the retail duplicates per arm (the lane CSEs it or materialises the `&&` as `mov eax,1`); a store-then-load the retail keeps ordered (`[esi+278h]` then `[esi+26Ch]`) that the lane hoists (volatile does not pin it); a float homed in the dead argument slot that retail `fld`s above the branch; `push ecx` frame slot reused as the `this` spill; the CDefPointer local re-read from its slot at every use (retail caches it in esi).
- Wave 24l (hand chunks 48, 49; agent chunk 47): a `??_G` deleting destructor needs its vtable pulled into the TU by a `new T` factory (a `delete` helper emits no vtable and the extractor grades the helper). COM interfaces (Direct3D / DirectInput / the font object) are `virtual ... __stdcall` slots with `this` pushed (`push edi ; push eax ; call [ecx+18h]`); the system record (0x009A4EC0) holds the render device at +0x60 -> +0x58 and the render statistics at +0x60 -> +8; `CreateIndexBuffer` is device slot 27, `Lock` / `GetDesc` are index-buffer slots 11 / 13. A call result consumed by a member chain (`Get()->a->b->Method(arg)`): the lane pushes the argument before the call, retail calls first = take the call into a local, then the chained call. The mapped pointer of `Lock` (and any out-pointer local) is homed in the dead size parameter slot by both compilers. An end pointer re-read per iteration = a local before the loop; `if (c) { ...; return true; } return false;` shares the `xor al,al` tail where the early `return false` inlines it. A member zeroed through its address register after the vtable (`lea edi,[esi+4] ... mov [edi],0`) is a body assignment while its neighbour is an initialiser. Retail vector push_back for 28/44-byte records = `new (last) T(value)` (the null-checked `rep movsd`) and `InsertGrow(last, value, SScalarTag(), 1, true)` (the tag homed in the dead argument slot, as the landed PushBackConstant family). A float-returning member forwarder still tail-jumps: `float v = call(); return v;` restores push/call/ret. `mov al,1`/`xor al,al` arms negated by `sete` = `return !Helper()` with Helper returning unsigned char. The out-parameter `GetInterface(&nav, 7)` template reproduces the single shared frame word and the garbage read on the absent path (do NOT null-initialise the out pointer). A `/Os` module needs `__forceinline` on counted-pointer releases and destructors (else 24 v 47). A `__declspec(novtable)` derived destructor stores the base vtable only when the base DECLARES an inline destructor. CDefPointeeBase is 4 bytes (one vptr). A hoisted `mov eax,1Bh` with an unfolded `mov eax,8 ; add eax,13h` arm = a default-initialised local plus a `__forceinline` helper holding the null case. Empty-class temporaries bound to `const&` in an initialiser list need a user-declared default constructor (else `xor eax,eax ; lea edi ; stosb` per temporary); a 1-byte temporary shared by four member constructors is one such allocator temporary. `add esp,4` of a `malloc` delayed to the end of a constructor: the stores before it are member-object constructors, the one after it a body assignment. `area->m_size += area->m_padding` (compound, the short converted first, the store sunk) orders the loads like retail. A size-capped bucket compared signed later = an `unsigned long` cap then a `long` index; STLport vector `Size()` must be written `if (m_pFirst == 0) return 0;` first for retail's block order. `fcomp ; test ah,41h ; jp` on the call edge = `!(x <= eps)`, not `x > eps`; `fabs` wants `#pragma intrinsic(fabs)` on a `double` prototype. Vtable slot counting: the polymorphic root's own `virtual v000()` IS slot 0, derived fillers start at v001. `lea ecx,[eax+N]` for the front-end manager's sprite before a tail jump = an inline accessor. A `ret 1Ch` setter whose eax is not `this` at the end is not a constructor. Lane gaps (parked): an empty tag temporary overlaid on a LIVE parameter slot (`lea eax,[esp+8] ; push eax ; lea edx,[esp+0Ch] ; push edx` for `std::find`'s value and tag; the lane CSEs one lea, pushes an uninitialised dword, or zero-fills its own slot; the SGI `find_if` with /Gr likewise); the RTM compiler materialising a vector slot address (`lea eax,[eax+ecx*4] ; mov eax,[eax]`, or an unused `lea` beside the load) that the lane folds for pointer/reference locals, `__forceinline` accessors and volatile elements; a by-value counted-pointer temporary whose vtable the lane keeps in edi across two virtual calls (the named-local form re-reads the vtable but reloads the local from its slot); two words stored before a derived vtable in argument order (+0x44 then +0x40) that the lane orders by member for every intermediate-base / setter / body form; the def-pointer frame word reused for a float before the release (62 v 52 x3, the QFE/RTM frame-slot sharing gap).
- Wave 24m (hand chunks 51, 53; agent chunk 50): VC7.1 emits `??_G<Class>@@QAEPAXI@Z` for a NON-polymorphic class deleted with `delete` (probe: `struct SA { CCharString a, b; }; void Kill(SA* p) { delete p; }`), so a deleting destructor with no vtable store is a plain class plus a `delete` helper; a deleting destructor that stores only the BASE vtable is a `__declspec(novtable)` derived class with a non-virtual destructor over a base whose inline destructor stores its vtable (the derived vtable folded into the base's by /OPT:ICF without RTTI), or, when the base chain has an out-of-line destructor two levels down, the base as the FIRST MEMBER of a non-polymorphic class; a class freed through the landscape memory manager has its own `static void operator delete`; a `novtable` class never emits `??_G` (nothing references it), a `virtual` destructor needs a `new T` factory, a non-virtual destructor in a polymorphic class (`virtual void v00(); ~T();`) keeps `??_G` out of the TU so the extractor's longest-substring match picks `??1`. Namespaced classes mangle as `??_GCObserver@NUISystem@@UAEPAXI@Z`. An unoptimised TU (`this` spilled to `[ebp-4]` and reloaded) is `#pragma optimize("", off)`. A by-value counted-pointer temporary read through `.Object` into a raw pointer releases before the member read, and its copy constructor/destructor share one null test. The CItem def release (`mov ecx,eax ; add eax,4 ; dec [eax] ; cmp [eax],0`) = the object copied to a second local for the virtual call, the count by address and the compare through a volatile cast, `__forceinline` under `/Os`. `shr eax,0Fh ; test al,1` = `(unsigned char)(flags >> 15) & 1` (a plain `(flags >> 15) & 1` folds to `test ah,ah ; jns`; a bit-field return type makes the call return through a hidden pointer). `lea ecx,[eax+50h]` for an embedded intelligent pointer = an inline accessor on the record. `cmp eax,esi` after two GetPItem calls = the second call's result on the LEFT of `==` with the first in a local. A counted-record assignment reads the other's two words into locals before the compare. `C3DVector operator+` (0x00C257E0) returns a class WITH a constructor through `__fastcall` (hidden pointer in ecx, `a` in edx, `b` on the stack; a POD result makes the lane pass the hidden pointer on the stack); a `sum` temporary then `position(sum)` gives the two frame copies. `dx*dx + dy*dy` with the left square first = `d = dx*dx; d += dy*dy;` (the `a + b` form multiplies the top of stack first). A scoped_ptr member as `this` of a null-tested virtual call uses the inline `operator->`; the retail vector iterator OBJECT (`*begin()`) fixes the this/out register pairing; `PCreature = CIntelligentPointer(p)` with a converting constructor and `operator=` reproduces the argument-first fetch / base ctor / vtable / SetPItem chain; the GetInterface template takes the MEMBER's address as its out pointer; `member = C2DVector(0.0f, 0.0f)` = an 8-byte frame temporary; two argument stores where retail stores arg2 first but loads arg1 first = write the source in LOAD order; ten 0xb0-byte CProcessedInput members = the inlined `dec ebx ; jne` vector-constructor loop. Retail CInputManager is 0x80 bytes (PDB vectors 16 -> 12; CDiskFileWin32 at +0x60, vtable 0x0122D06C: Open slot 1, Close 2, Write 4); CInputProcessJumpingAndRolling is 0x54 bytes. Lane gaps (parked): one compare serving both a clamp and a zero test (`cmp [eax],ebx ; jge ; mov [eax],ebx ; cmp ebx,ebx ; jne`); a sum stored, divided from the register, then overwritten (the lane reloads or dead-store-eliminates); two separate function exits the lane tail-merges; a float homed in the dead argument slot with the position held in edi; the malloc'd word kept in esi across the call; a `lea` scheduled before two vtable stores; an LTCG callee assumed to preserve ecx (0x005C150B, skipped).
- Wave 24n (hand chunk 54; agent chunk 52): a vtable store BEFORE a run of zero stores that END with an import call means an embedded state struct with an inline constructor (`SState() : a(0), b(0), c(0) { InitializeCriticalSection(&s); }`, 0x009D5EB0); a `push_back(const T&)` argument that retail copies into the dead argument slot (`mov [ebp+8],eax ; lea eax,[ebp+8]`) must be an RVALUE in the source (an inline accessor returning by value), an lvalue member binds directly; VC6-era `vector::erase(begin(), end())` has NO empty-range test (`__fastcall _Move(last, last, first, tag, false)` + `__fastcall _Destroy(kept, last, tag)` + `last = kept`); a `resize(n, T())` whose zero temporary is stored with IMMEDIATES after the pushes is a pair of floats (a long pair gives `xor eax,eax` before the pushes); a 0x2AAAAAAB multiply with `sar edx,2` is a divide by 24 (12 is `sar edx,1`); `dec dword ptr [ecx+n] ; jne ; mov eax,[ecx] ; push 1 ; call [eax]` with no null test before the call is a plain virtual `Destroy(1)` on a local copy, not `delete`; a counted-pointer `operator=` reads BOTH source fields into locals before the block compare (retail loads the object between the `cmp` and its `je`); a `/Os` + `y`-off hidden-pointer temporary from a virtual getter (`CDefPointer p = GetOwner()`) lands in the frame slot with the counted-def release inlined. - Wave 24o (hand chunks 56, 57; agent chunk 55): a hidden-pointer struct (C3DVector) built field by field in a NAMED local and returned through a memberwise user copy constructor folds into x87 stores (`fxch st(2)` then three `fstp`): VC7.1 has no NRVO but the copy of a fully computed local costs nothing, a value constructor evaluates its arguments right to left, a POD copies through the frame; a store that precedes BOTH the derived vtable store and the first member's `lea` is the inline constructor of a `__declspec(novtable)` polymorphic base (a non-polymorphic base puts the `lea` first); stores that precede the derived vtable store in a CBaseClassNonCopyable-derived constructor are a SECOND, non-polymorphic base with an inline constructor; a counter member published to a global and then used must be read into a LOCAL first (`shift = m_shift; g = shift; ... >> shift`), else the lane re-reads the member after the global store; a loop-invariant expression written INSIDE the loop is hoisted after the entry test (written before the loop it precedes the test); three vtable stores at +0, +4, +0x18 = a primary polymorphic base plus two secondary ones; `??_G` through `new T(args)` needs the constructor INLINE in the TU (an out-of-line constructor leaves only the factory symbol); SGI `__median(a, b, c, comp)` passes the empty comparator by value (`__fastcall`, two register + two stack slots, `ret 8`); a 4-byte iterator with user constructors passed by value is built in the argument area (`push ecx ; mov edx,esp ; mov [edx],eax`) and returned by hidden pointer into a frame slot; `__declspec(novtable)` on a derived class whose destructor is empty removes its own vtable store so only the base's inline destructor stores one; a `__thiscall` member that never reads `this` still gives `ret N` (the tree BuyNode twins labelled NodeAllocate). - Wave 24p (hand chunks 59, 60): a `/Os` module copies every member through eax (`push 6 ; pop eax`, unaligned loop heads); an SSE `__m128` returned by hidden pointer must be wrapped in a struct (a bare `__m128` returns in xmm0); `#pragma pack(1)` for unaligned members (+0x76, +7); a 2D delta built in a NAMED local returned by memberwise copy keeps both differences on the FPU stack; a `volatile` next pointer keeps retail's double read; a row whose bytes span two functions is skipped, not authored. VC7.1 `list::clear` advances the loop variable BEFORE destroying the node (`node = next; next = next->next; destroy; free(node)` -- the `next = node->next; ...; node = next` form swaps esi/ebx); sequential `if (child->X()) return true;` tests place the shared `mov al,1` exit after the third test where `a || b || c` puts it last; `if (A && B) return true; return false;` keeps two exits where two `if (!x) return false` give `setne`; a map `find` written as the ternary `(it == end || key < it->key) ? end : it` inside an inline member (the caller re-reading `end`) reproduces the `jb`/`ja` order and the second `m_last` load; the CCharString `operator==` must take REFERENCES so both data blocks load inside the compare; `this == p->m_a ? p->m_b : p->m_a` with `this` on the left for `cmp ecx,esi`; a `bool` forwarded to a `long` parameter is `movzx`; a destructor that uses a member pointer twice (explicit `~T()` then `operator delete`) copies it into a LOCAL (edi) -- the member form re-reads it; three 16-byte POD records forwarded by value copy with `movsd` x4 each only under `optimize("s",on)` (the speed form uses register pairs); a constructor storing a dword after two later bytes puts that dword in the BODY (initialiser lists store in declaration order). Lane gaps parked: an inline counted-pointer destructor that retail routes through the out-of-line `??_G` with 0 (0x005BB190: inline, `virtual`, `novtable` and `delete`-helper forms all give `??1`); a loop-invariant reference argument hoisted into ebp with an unaligned loop head (0x00A15100); the `find` tag temporary over the live key slot (0x00A76C70); a recursive call reusing ecx across an LTCG callee (0x00A77420).
- **Skipped-ledger pieces land only after a manifest row** (wave 25, chunk 901): `verify_and_land.py` refuses any start absent from `rebuild/manifest/functions.tsv` as OUTSIDE_MANIFEST, and the manifest knew only the original multi-function row -- 21 of 34 pieces were thrown away on the first landing. `skipped_ledger.py to-chunk` now adds each piece start (vtable-slot evidence from rtti-vtables.tsv, else the split itself). Also: `q.py` takes the FIRST batch oracle row for an address, so a stale multi-function row in an older chunk oracle shadows the split piece (delete it first).
- **STLport `vector::operator[]` is `*(begin() + n)`** (wave 25 retry): modelling it as `first[n]` folds the element address into one `lea` and CSEs the load; retail (size TU) keeps the scaled index in its own register (`shl ecx,2 ; cmp [ecx+eax],0 ; mov edx,[ecx+eax]`) and re-reads the element for a `__fastcall` argument. Three parked CTCInventory rows flipped to MATCH with the inline `begin()` form.
- **Wave 25 agent lessons (chunks 61–111)**: VC7.1 uses the register-clobber facts of callees compiled EARLIER in the same TU (`this` kept in edx across a call, hoisted loop counts, no ecx reload) -- define the callee body above the function (`#pragma auto_inline(off)`); the STLport `_Vector_base` + `_STLP_alloc_proxy` model fixes the "24i" scheduling shapes (zero words after the vtable store, `mov ecx,edi` placement, a malloc's `add esp,4`); size-TU markers: `or byte ptr [m],imm`, `fld ; push ecx ; fstp [esp]`, one shared zero register, `lea` address formation, `movsd x3 ; movsw` record copies; a virtual first declared by a secondary base compiles with `this` at that subobject (negative member offsets); overloaded virtuals sit in reverse declaration order; after `GetSubDef(def)` a hand-written pointer-local release gives `dec [esi+4] ; jne` unless the pointer is tested right after the load; `GetWorld()->StaticFn()` still emits the GetWorld call; a `(float)` cast returned straight from an intrinsic tail-jumps where retail keeps `call ; ret` (name the local); the search cursor declared before its limit takes edx.
- The lander keys sources by file name, not address: a re-authored row that is already landed gets a second `*_<addr>.cpp`. After landing, scan `rebuild/src/compiled` for duplicate address suffixes and keep the file named like the catalog row (hygiene 2026-10-15, 24 pairs).
- FP compares: `fcomp mem ; fnstsw ; test ah,5 ; jp` is `if (x < c)`; `test ah,1 ; jne` is what `>=` produces. Read the sense from the jump, not from which branch returns 1 (009576d0).
- `dx*dx + dy*dy` in one expression squares the FP top (dy) first; retail's dx-first order is `float d = dx*dx; d += dy*dy;` (009576d0, same family as the 00A90860 statement-by-statement sum).
- Wave 25 chunks 122–127 lessons: a CCharString local shares an inlined lookup key's frame word only inside its own `{}` block; VC7.1 uses callee clobber knowledge only when the callee is defined out of class above the caller under `auto_inline(off)`; CIntelligentPointer is 8 bytes (vptr + item); retail CCreatureActionBase is 0xa8 and CCombatActionBase 0x114 (PDB 0xb4/0x12c), so derived members sit 12/24 bytes below the engine-header offsets; the 0x006924B0 animation lookup takes an IMPLICIT CCharString conversion temporary; `f() ? false : true` gives the 8-bit `neg/sbb/inc` tail where `!f()` widens to eax; a float stored with three direct dword moves into a packed member is the memberwise user copy ctor.
- Unchecked GetInterface is common (13 of 40 rows in chunk 129): retail ignores the out-parameter result (`GetInterface(&p, T); p->Call();`), so the absent arm reads the never-written out pointer from the lookup key's frame word and VC duplicates the following call into both arms. An out-pointer getter that must share the single `push ecx` word with a later inlined lookup key goes in an inline accessor; a function-scope local takes its own word (0087cd50). A 3-word frame holding one float is `C3DVector v(0, 0, x)` with one used component (008c0790).
- A chained `GetWorld()->Manager->Call(args)` pushes the arguments first and fetches the manager last; when retail fetches the world between the register arguments and the pushes, wrap the call in an inline helper whose body starts with `CWorld* world = GetWorld();` (008491e0, retry of the chunk 130 park).
- Chunk 131 lessons: STLport's pair has an explicit memberwise copy ctor, so a 12/16-byte member retail copies straight from the argument slots needs a memberwise user copy ctor over dword members; STLport `_Destroy(first, last)` takes its bounds by value so the end is cached in a register; a counted pointer returned by value whose object retail reads back from the return slot must be a named local (release by hand through an inline guard, 009C95E0); `if (x >= 0) m = x;` on a float member reloads the value, an inline helper taking it by value keeps it on the FPU; member functions return even an 8-byte POD through the hidden pointer; a `/Gr` `__fastcall` template returning a POD takes its hidden pointer on the stack with the first argument in ecx; declare `strcmp` by hand when the same file needs a dllimport `_stricmp` (`<string.h>` gives C2375).
- Chunk 132 lessons: a memberwise copy of a POD-like record needs an inline user copy ctor (the implicit one is `rep movsd`); walk a terminated table with an indexed `for (long i = 0; t[i].F != -1; ++i)`; `(fov*0.5f)*(double)TWO_PI` keeps two multiplies; a CEnginePrimitiveHandle's Data pointer must be a member object with its own inline ctor so the shared zero register lands after the handle's vtable store; a stat-bar alpha is an `unsigned char` parameter; `if (x > 0)` on an unsigned gives `jbe`; when the presence test is the out-of-line bitset::test, split it into an inline HasInterface plus a `__forceinline` lookup returning the component; chained status calls want nested `if (result >= 0)` blocks; a by-value CRGBColour built in the argument area needs a user copy ctor. New lane gaps: separate frame slots for two by-value call temporaries; a dead LARGE_INTEGER high-half store retail keeps.
- (wave 25, chunk 134) A frame slot that is zeroed and never read is a dead temporary VC7.1 keeps for `member = C4DVector(0,0,0,0)` inside an inline constructor body.
- (wave 25, chunk 134) `mov al,[x] ; shr al,7 ; and al,1` is an `unsigned short` bit field; `unsigned char` drops the `and`, `unsigned long` loads the whole dword.
- (wave 25, chunk 134) Four `fstp` stores R,G,B,A after loads in A,B,G,R order = an inline four-argument setter (arguments evaluate right to left). `a = b = f()` stores `a` last.
- (wave 25, chunk 134) A counted-pointer reset must go through an inline `Reset()` member or its zero stores sink below the next block; a key retail copies into a dead argument slot is the by-value parameter of a `__forceinline` helper.
- (wave 25, chunk 134) `for_each(..., bind2nd(mem_fun(&CThing::SetInLimbo), flag))` passes the 8-byte binder on the stack and pushes the member pointer as an immediate.
- (wave 25, chunk 135) A deleting dtor passing `xor edx,edx` to a `__fastcall` class operator delete = inline usual `operator delete(p)` forwarding `(p, 0)` to a two-argument `(void*, long)` form; a `(void*, size_t)` form passes the element size instead.
- (wave 25, chunk 135) Stores before a derived vtable store in an inlined ctor belong to a `__declspec(novtable)` intermediate base; as the derived class's own initialisers they land after the vtable store. Large primitive ctors used by `new T` factories need `__forceinline` on every ctor in the chain.
- (wave 25, chunk 135) A colour copy that loads all four bytes before storing any is a copy ctor calling inline `Set(r,g,b,a)`; an identity matrix with descending zero stores then the diagonal is a top-down fill followed by m[8], m[4], m[0]; a 4-float vector whose w is computed before x is an inline `Set(x,y,z,w)`.
- (wave 25, chunk 135) `dec [ecx+N] ; jne ; push 1 ; call [eax]` with no second test is a local pointer copy plus `delete`. Six of 40 propagated labels in this chunk were other classes: check the vtable store, callers and PDB size before trusting a label.
- (wave 25, chunk 136) Engine primitive ctors: the PDB signatures `??0CEnginePrimitiveX@@QAE@_K...` give the parameter order and the leading `_K` handle is never stored; the type immediate identifies the class (the catalog swaps Light 0x642000 / ParticleLine 0xAFE220 / Line 0x7324A0 labels). 0xA02D20 is CHandleBasedMemoryPool's ctor (vtable 0x129C964).
- (wave 25, chunk 136) `fldz ; fstp` for a zero float member marks a size TU (`optimize("s", on)`, plus `("y", off)` with a frame pointer). A FrameEnd with odd offsets runs with `this` at a secondary base: find the subobject from the ctor's second vtable store.
- (wave 25, chunk 136) Read the manager into a local before declaring a zeroed chain local and VC sinks the zero stores below the argument pushes. `GFGetSystemManager()->member->Call(args)` with the call before the pushes needs a system-manager local. A counted pointer's `operator->` loads into eax, `.Data->` into edx.
- (wave 25, chunk 136) `mov eax,[esp+N] ; mov [esp+N],eax` (self copy) comes from a named local assigned from a default-constructed temporary of the same type. Unaligned word loads (+0xe) need `#pragma pack(1)` with explicit pads. CEngineSubPrimitiveBase is `__declspec(novtable)`; the pool allocator 0xBEB540 is `static void* __fastcall operator new`.
- (wave 25, chunk 138) A struct temporary retail keeps half on the FPU and half in the frame (x/y via `fld`, z as a dword copy) is a by-value getter result such as `C3DVector GetCentre() const`; reading the member directly folds into memory operands.
- (wave 25, chunk 138) `CCountedPointer<T> p(new T); return p;` reproduces the merged `inc ; mov eax,ecx ; dec eax` copy-out and release (VC7.1 has no NRVO). A value kept in a register while its address is also passed is a copy: `unsigned long k = key; LowerBound(&k); ... key < it->key`.
- (wave 25, chunk 138) `m_cur = m_def = x` on floats gives `fst ; fstp`; a single float member assignment is an integer move. A clamp where fldz/fld1 meet in one shared `fstp` is `if/else if` under `optimize("s")`. Retail's A,B,G,R conversion order means `CRGBFloatColour(R,G,B,A)` (args evaluate right to left).
- (wave 25, chunk 138) Label corrections: 0x512C70 is a no-arg /Gr counted-pointer factory (not CreateNewBankMap), 0x9D58D0 CBankFileAsync::CreateEntry, 0xB40000 CloseStaticMap, 0xB42C70 DeleteAllMaps, 0x6B3600 SetThemeWeight, 0x530EC0 is a def getter not a ctor, 0x993EB0 "WriteCBYTE" writes a long, 0xF35B30 is the entity script's IsDone.
- (wave 25, chunk 137) Stores running from the highest address down are a chained `a = b = c = 0.0f;` (right-to-left evaluation); this is how 0xB50510 builds its identity matrix.
