- Wave 25, chunk 188: an intelligent pointer passed through an inline `operator T*() { return GetPItem(); }` is fetched before all the plain arguments (fixed four new-expression rows); constants stored out of declaration order are ctor-body assignments; `tc->P = target` through an inline by-value operator= builds the parameter copy in a dead frame slot; a dtor that never re-stores its vtable is `__declspec(novtable)` with the member's inline dtor `__forceinline`; an if/else of two calls tail-merges into `push 1 / push 0`, a ternary argument gives `mov/xor`; member-by-member assignment of a default-constructed C3DVector copies dwords, the 3-float ctor goes through the FPU; the inline `CCharString::operator==("")` materialising its bool with `sete` is a new lane gap (three spell ctor rows). Labels: 0x8CE570 CCreatureAction_BattleChargeMiss ctor (vtable 0x012847C4), 0xAF10E0 NParticleEngine::CParticleEmitter dtor (deleting dtor 0xAF10C0, vtable 0x0129F348, graphics row), 0x7C5440/0x7C3EA0 SetAICast setters, 0x9AD390 GetDefIndexFromName, 0x8574D0 SetAnimationName.
- Codex review (2026-10-19): `RELOCATION_MATCH` proves bytes, not callee identity -- a source can call the wrong overload (`CPersistContext::Transfer` float 0x431061 vs long 0x431102), `::operator new` where retail calls the malloc import 0xBFEA0E, scalar `??2/??3` where retail calls the array forms 0xBFEB22/0xBFEB1C, or bind one extern symbol to two retail callees/float pools, and still match; audit the relocation targets (reviewers' `relocmap`) before accepting a row. A fixture that re-implements the algorithm or only prints PASS is not a test; 40 of 552 Codex tests did that.
- x87 compare masks (Codex, verified): after `FCOMP/FUCOMPP ; FNSTSW AX`, AH&0x45 is 0x00 greater, 0x01 less, 0x40 equal, 0x45 unordered; `TEST AH,5 ; JP reject` accepts only ordered less-than, `TEST AH,0x41 ; JNE reject` only ordered greater-than, `TEST AH,0x44 ; JNP reject` accepts not-equal including NaN -- read the load order and branch target before choosing the source predicate, and read the float-pool bytes (relocation masking hides the literal).
- Codex lessons kept: VC7.1 lane DDK `<new>` lacks an ordinary `operator delete[]` declaration, so `delete[] p` silently emits scalar `??3` while still byte-matching (declare `void operator delete[](void*)`); an argument pushed before a virtual call may belong to the following direct call; implicit compiler-generated C3DVector assignment emits the shared `add this,14h` destination where a handwritten three-field assignment uses displaced stores; retail `0082d2d0` is `CThingBuilding::PeekPSimDefinition`, not `PeekPMaterial`; `004C7770` is `CThing::SetAsActivated(bool)` (native byte 0x91 bit 3), its canonical label is still unrepaired.
- Verifier (2026-10-19): `reloc_identity.py` rejects a byte-match whose relocations bind one symbol to two retail targets, one target to two symbols, or an import thunk under the wrong name; Ghidra label disagreements are warnings because many labels are propagated (0x4C73D0 is `CThing::GetPosition`, labelled `CVertexBufferWin32::DoSizeof`). Run `reloc_audit.py` over any batch before trusting a RELOCATION_MATCH.
- Codex hold rework (agent990, 2026-10-19): placeholder callee names (A/B/C, TransferAlt, ReleaseNode, CBase, `*Calls`, `Nearby*`) hide wrong callees -- name every extern after the retail function the relocation resolves to; hard-coded vtable/string immediates should be real polymorphic members or literals; the engine C3DVector needs a user copy ctor for implicit copies to byte-match; VC7.1 folds literal float sums (0.3f + 1e-4f), so two .rdata operands mean two named const globals; WriteLong(long) is 0x993EB0 and WriteULong is 0x993EA0; 0x99A2D0/0x99A2E0 are the CBaseClass ctor/dtor, 0x99A380 the CBaseObject ctor; retail def layouts sit at PDB offset - 4 (0x3C-byte base) and STLport containers are 12 bytes. Test pitfalls: arm malloc/free spies only around the call, compare raw bytes not `== (bool)0xcd`, hold float expectations in a float local.
- (2026-10-19, relocation-identity repairs, 235 rows) The lane `<new>` lacks `operator new[]`/`delete[]`: declare them or VC7.1 binds `delete[] p` to scalar `??3` (same bytes, wrong import). Under /EHsc an `operator delete[]` replacing `free` must be declared `throw()` or the EH state code changes. A `free`/`operator delete` spy in a test records only while armed and never frees (no `_free_base` in VC7.1 libc). RELOCATION_MATCH hides addend differences: the 23 static counted-pointer releases stored the count word before the object word and 00a0d410 read vtable slot 4 not 3; the identity audit's base-target check exposed both. No ICF in retail: two names bound to one address are one function. Name destructor callees from vtable RTTI, not propagated labels. Label corrections in `ghidra_out/labels_identity_corrections_2026-10-19.tsv` (0x693EF0 ~CCreatureActionBase, 0x99B510 ~CWideString, 0x99A300 ~CBaseClassNonCopyable, 0x431020/61/102, 0x43314A CPersistContext transfers, 0x44C6B0 CDefinitionManager::Get, 0x6E7510 a distinct frame getter). A test that `#include`s the repo source by relative path cannot run under the verifier (it compiles from a scratch dir): inline the source (00495fe0).

HasTC + a one-ternary GetTC leaves the component in ecx for the next `this`. A polymorphic thing model keeps its vptr in the root class or `this` shifts by 4. - (wave 25, retry 8: 17/129 parked rows recovered) Formulations that turned a DIFFER into a match: take a child-handle reference before a manager-table lookup (fixes which register a global lands in); copy a position into a named C2DVector local via a user copy ctor then `SetXY(p.X, p.Y)` to send floats through the FPU; PDB ctors with named locals in their own `{}` block give the extra frame slots; write a length-squared as `s = Z*Z; s += X*X; s += Y*Y;`; an inline by-value `WriteLong(file, v)` fixes which dead argument slot each value homes in; a holder type whose dtor is a direct `--count` gives `dec [edi+4] ; jne` while the getter's temporary keeps the long release; `(a && !b) || (!a && b)` lets both arms share one call block; `break` out of `do {} while (0)` cross-jumps early returns into the single epilogue; `if (IsDone()) return;` before the body keeps a second timer-dtor tail; split an `||` chain into separate `if (x) return false;` to inline the shared false exit; a zero-initialised result assigned inside one combined test keeps the zero in ebx; `if (i < n) { zero stores; do {...} while (++i < n); }` for unhoisted zero stores; pass CAnimInit as a temporary rather than a named local; a value-initialised member (`CBlankEntry() : Entry() {}`) gives one zero-fill; an inline `GetJoystick(n)` accessor puts the device straight into ecx; bind `CRegion& region` first, then build the counted pointer from `region.m_pDef.Data`. Lane gap confirmed: an inlined null-tested counted-pointer release never reuses the `dec` flags (`dec ; jne`), whatever the spelling; out of line it does. `g.sh --test` in the agent dirs passes `--test` to q.py as a leaf: run tests through the verifier's `behaviour_of` instead.

Keep the chunk files, or check a new cut against `pending/*/differ/` before launching.
- (wave 25, chunk 200) A jump table with no range check is `default: __assume(0)`; VC7.1 lays case bodies out in source order, so write cases in retail's order. A traits local whose ctor only zeroes a member gets that store hoisted above the switch jump. An inline STLport `clear()` must go through `erase(first, last)` with real parameters. `switch` with only `case 2` + `default` gives `sub eax,2 ; je`; an `if` gives `cmp`. A dropped by-value `ReadULong()` skips the read; a named address-taken local keeps a dead store. A by-value C3DVector argument to a dot helper loads all three components onto the x87 stack first. `i != 3` gives `jne`, `i < 3` gives `jl`. A script handle declared at function scope after an early return gets its own frame slot. `if (ok) {...; return true;} return false;` puts the false epilogue last. 0xC1C330 uses an LTCG custom register convention (lane gap). Generated Transfer rows come from `gen/` scripts in the agent dir (regenerate, don't hand-edit). 0x129BA3C is 1e-4f (`g_epsilon_0129ba3c`, not `g_fZero`); 0x13B86A0 is `g_pMainGameComponent`. Labels: `ghidra_out/labels_chunk200_corrections_2026-10-19.tsv`.

Several temporaries in one `||` chain: VC builds the constant-folded mask (`mov ebx,1/3/7`) itself. Separate `IsActiveThreadTerminating(); return;` arms reproduce the jump past `mov ecx,esi`. A difference used twice in one condition must be written twice (a named local rotates registers). Every STLport container is 12 bytes in retail (vector<bool> included): CQ_FireHeartScript members line up with Ego_r.pdb minus 0x14 and minus 4 per container. 0x449B60 holds three bodies (SKIP). Gap stated precisely: VC7.1 expands an inline callee at every site below a cost band, exactly once in the next band (block-end site first, early return calls the out-of-line copy), never above; cost is counted pre-DSE; the lane's QFE-4035 inliner starts the once band at a higher callee cost than retail's RTM compiler (00d17090, 0066bd00, 00e32200). x87 operand rank: only body-level sums over one object follow source order in both compilers. - (2026-10-20) The retail compiler is cl 13.10.3077 (Rich header; five objects 4035; linker 3052). The free Visual C++ Toolkit 2003 is a pre-RTM 13.10.3052 (discarded). The Linux lane now has the real 3077 (`$S/wine/vc71rtm`, BUILDING.md): it fixes the parked `/GS` rows (stack-cookie prologue; 5 landed) and nothing else: inventory frame-slot sharing, zero-register reuse, the inline-once destructor band and the biased /GS ebp frame differ identically under 3077, so they are formulation gaps. /GS test links need `LINK=bufferoverflowu.lib` on every compiler.

Trim before a direct call. Also: a parked `differ/` row can be stale once a ledger chunk lands the address (009b98b0); check the catalog before re-landing.

- **CScriptThing inline-once dtor rule, measured (chunk 274 advisor, 35 grades):** the inline decision is FUNCTION-WIDE with two regimes. No "proper" loop (the while body's lexical end unreachable — every path leaves by continue/return/break) → EVERY dtor site is expanded. Any proper loop (body end reachable — even a code-free `do{}while(0)` around one arm) → at most ONE expansion, at the loop-test exit for `while(!IATT())`/`do..while(!IATT())` loops, else the first top-level site. Lever for F2 watch loops that inline only at the loop exit: `if (!IsActiveThreadTerminating()) { do { ...; Game->NewScriptFrame(); } while (!IsActiveThreadTerminating()); }` — also yields the xor-reg zero register (00e6a340 landed). Retail rows mixing "4 inline + 1 out-of-line" (00e53d90, 00e36390, 00ed9790, 00f09260, 00f09950, 00e1fcf0, 00e1fef0, 00e6b750, 00d97170) are unreachable: park. **Superseded 2026-10-22 (advisor b18): the mixed inline/out-of-line split IS reachable with in-body `#pragma inline_depth(0)` regions, see the b18 line below; all 13 rows named here landed.**

A never-written temporary (resize `T()`) shares a live local's frame word only if its address reaches no extern; to find how deep the chain of real bodies must go, probe with a noinline stub that reads the argument.

Unsolved residue: the string temp takes the dead parameter slot instead of its own `[ebp-0Ch]`.

It is not universal (00c0b570 got worse). Reuse the dying operand as the result (`offset -= axis*along; offset.MagSquared()`) to home spills in retail's dead words (00815ce0). - int3-after-noreturn rows LANDED (2026-10-22, 7 rows: 00a0d1a0, 00a0d200, 00567d60, 00a5b260, 00567d00, 009a7ca0, 00567400): grade and land against an N+1 oracle row read straight from Fable.exe, where byte N is the 0xCC directly after the final `call _CxxThrowException` / `_M_throw_length_error` and the next function starts right after it. Never take padding past the build's own length. The verifier prints RELOC_UNCHECKED for these (VC7.1 puts the int3 under its own `$L` label, so the object block is N bytes); check relocation identity by hand against the N-byte block. - AdvB3 findings (2026-10-22): VC7.1 merges a bare-return exit into whichever identical tail it lays out next to, and source lowering order does not move that choice (0096ee90, 00d08b40 parked at 1-2 bytes: "tail pairing" family); `this` vs a sub-object in ebp/edi follows how many times an inline accessor is used; inline-expanded temps are numbered after parse-time temps (scratch-register naming); extern callees handed `&local` pessimise later inline loads, so give them same-TU out-only bodies. A masked byte diff that shows WHICH tail a branch targets (udiff's `jne X` normalisation hides it): $S/w22/retry_shared/advisor/tools/mdiff.py.
- **Sunk store before the loads that follow it** (004b4f30 SetQuestMasterDataPersistScript): when retail keeps `Dirty = true` ahead of the sort operand loads, bind `CPersistMap* const& self = this;` and read the operands through `self->`; the may-alias loads pin the store (moving the store itself never worked, 9 forms).
- **Float pair loaded before a pointer is formed** (0083c990 CThingSwitch::TriggerSetPlayerAreaName): bind `const C3DVector& pos = hero->GetPhysicsPosition();` as a named reference inside its own `if (hero && !hero->Dead) { ... }` block before `ContainsPoint(C2DVector(pos))`, so both floats load ahead of `add eax,0Ch`. - **Raw float read from a byte buffer** (00a42df0 CLipSyncDataBank::NotifyEntryAdded): `float length; memcpy(&length, data.GetData(), sizeof(float));` then store it, instead of an integer local punned through `*(float*)&bits`. A union pun, a named entry reference and a store helper made no difference; a `float&` to the member collapses to an integer store.
- **Sequenced float sums beat VC7.1's x87 operand order** (AdvB3, 00a571a0, at 654v654): `float s = a*a; s += b*b;` keeps the left-first order that `a*a + b*b` loses; swapping the operands gets normalised back by the compiler. `*out = cond ? true : false` gives the byte bool `mov al,1 / xor al,al`.
- **Insert with a by-value name and the time read first** (AdvB3, 00a4ebb0, at 604v604): `float time = e->Time; AddTimingEvent(e->Name, time);`, with the inline member taking `CCharString` by value and inserting a `pair<CCharString,float>`. - **Chunk 281 lessons:**
  - Copy a counted pointer member by member and take the raw pointer before `++Info->RefCount`, so Data is not reloaded after the increment.
  - An empty exception class with a user ctor makes `throw E()` copy an uninitialised byte from the dead argument slot, as retail does.
  - An intrinsic `memcmp(a, b, 4)` gives retail's ID-compare shape.
  - For an STLport list insert at the end, name the old tail first: `prev = head->_M_prev`.

Plain `inline` is not expanded under /Os, so use __forceinline in size TUs.

An inline by-value copy is written as a tagged inline ctor, `CScriptThing(Me, CScriptThing::InlineCopy())`, which emits no symbol of its own.

- **Chunk 285 lessons:**
  - A large inline matrix helper needs `__forceinline` to inline at all, and a forced full matrix product is not trimmed.
  - VC7.1 reorders commutative x87 terms on its own, so source order cannot fix them (00b47390).
  - CRGBFloatColour `Set()` gives direct constant stores; a ctor temporary goes through the frame.
  - `size() > 0 ? _M_start : (T*)this` gives retail's `ja`; plain `size() ?` gives `jne`.
  - A frame-size miss of exactly N bytes usually means a callee-built object is larger than modelled (CCompressor's body is 0x54).
  - An STLport tag argument with no zero store in retail means the tag struct has an empty user ctor.
  - A class-scope `static void* __fastcall operator new(unsigned int)` for the 0x00BEB540 pool allocator is accepted by the lander; only the global operator new must not be redeclared.

- **Retail calling the MSVCR71 malloc thunk 0x00BFEA0E is RELOC_IDENTITY for operator new** (chunk 283, 00846410): write `malloc(sizeof(T))`, as free() replaces operator delete.

  - When retail inlines the first push_back and calls the second out of line, write the first as a __forceinline STLport body.

- **Mixed inline/out-of-line ~CScriptThing, solved (2026-10-22, chunk 282 00ef12f0 then advisor b18, 13 rows: 00f09260 00f09950 00e1fcf0 00e1fef0 00e6b750 00d97170 00715110 00dcdc50 00ef84d0 00e53d90 00e36390 00ed9790 00ee6a40):** VC7.1 decides each dtor expansion at its site under the inline depth in force there. (1) Make the dtor (or vector<CScriptThing> dtor) `__forceinline`: plain `inline` is all-or-nothing function-wide, so the pragma has nothing to split. (2) Put `#pragma inline_depth(0)` ... `#pragma inline_depth()` inside the body around exactly the exits retail calls out of line (0x004AA840); a `return` is decided where it is written, the fall-through exit at the closing brace, so depth 0 just before the final `}` outlines only that exit. (3) Keep regions narrow: depth 0 also blocks inline ctors; start after declarations whose ctor must expand; to keep a filter's member dtor out of line while the filter dtor expands, put depth 0 around the filter class definition. (4) Companion shape: `for (;;) { if (IsActiveThreadTerminating()) return; ... }` (the loop exit skips the trailing terminate test; expansion register and Data/Info zero-store order follow retail); where retail lays dead/out-of-line exits out of source order put the dead branch first or write `if (alive) {...} else {...}`. (5) Inline `CCharString == "lit"` is the landed 0x00E6A000 nested model (`CBasicString<char>::operator==`, null arm `if (strcmp(text, "") == 0) return true; return false;`). 00f09950's parked source had a wrong `break` after the hit.

STLport push_back wants the insert shape with `n`/`prev` locals; VC7.1 canonicalises 3-term dot products (all 48 source orders of 00a89e40's E11 give the same code). Open: retail keeps the CIntelligentPointer vtable address in ebx/ebp (00972cc0, 009456a0, 00a60d40).

Give the callee a same-TU body that only reads the argument; for a class-template member it must be a non-template explicit specialisation under `#pragma auto_inline(off)`, since a template member body is compiled after the caller and never seen.

Destroy()` puts the temp in edx; inline `AddRef()`/`Release()` members on the def class give retail's ecx and can flip the whole function's esi/edi assignment. STLport `clear()` written `erase(begin(), end())` fixes scratch-register cascades. Out-param GetInterface lands the component in eax, a returning GetTC in ecx. Holding a counted-pointer element by reference changes register assignment without changing the instruction stream (diagnostic for the 507 reload-after-region gap).

- **`for(;;){ if (IsActiveThreadTerminating()) break; ... }` vs `while(!...)`** (00ebf1e0, b49): the top-break form gives retail's hoisted vtable load above the inner jne and the eax/edx split across duplicated CTimer-dtor exits; try it when a script loop's exit copies differ only in register.
- **CIVCountedPointer dtor nulls inside the null test** (`if (Object) { Release(); Object = 0; }`, 0070cad0/0070cc70): a DIFFER with no mnemonic lines can be two je targets 8 bytes apart — compare raw jump targets.
- **Tail-jump refused for an address-taken tag** (00567e80, b50): give the tail-callee (STLport range `append`) a same-TU `template` noinline member body with the tag unnamed; VC7.1 then homes the tag in the dead `last` slot and emits retail's `lea/mov/jmp` sibling jump.
- **Shared `return false` block placement** (00bfc600, b51): `return f() ? true : false;` as the last of a chain of early-returning tests puts the shared false block after the last test; writing it as one more `if (!f()) return false; return true;` puts it right after the first test, as retail. Goto layouts and else-if chains do not.
- **Empty-tag rule also stops a pair temporary escaping** (00cbd5d0 CVectorMap InsertEntry, b52): template `_M_insert_overflow` (unnamed tag) plus a member-initialiser `CCountedPointer` copy ctor.
- **Script by-value CScriptThing args from a vector** (00dfc920, b53): the 00d16bb0 PImp member model plus `operator[]` as `*(begin() + n)` (not `_M_start[n]`) makes the copies read [offset+start] straight into the argument area as retail.
- **Named local inside the nested test block** (0089a880, b54): `CScriptThing* fromItem = from.PImp.Data;` declared inside the distance-test `if` makes VC evaluate the whole `from` chain before loading the call's `this` (retail ecx/eax rotation); a one-expression `!(thing == from)` interleaves the loads, and a named local before the `if` hoists them.
- **Untouched frame bytes under optimize-off** (00a44880, b55): VC7.1 still expands `__forceinline` with optimisation off and gives the inlinee's locals slots BELOW the caller's temporaries; an unused local array in a forceinline helper reproduces retail's never-touched gap (a caller-level array lands above). Flagged as a frame model: the original inlinee's contents are unknown.
- **Counted-pointer release on a local copy** (00b66c10, D1): `CFrameData* old = Data; if (--old->RefCount == 0) old->DeleteThis(); Data = 0;` gives retail's `dec [ecx+4] ; jne`; releasing through the member re-reads it after the dec store.
- **Block-scoped named CCharString argument** (005be8f3, D1): naming the empty-name argument in a block sequences its ctor before a member load the temporary form hoists above it.

- **x87 commutative operand order follows load-node creation order** (008cad50, D1): the later-created load is pushed first, and each (variable, member) load is created where it is first referenced in source — so an EARLIER expression (e.g. the dot product) decides a later cross product's operand order. `float dot = d.X*f.X + d.Y*f.Y; dot += d.Z*f.Z;` gave retail's Y,X,+,Z,+ and the Y-first cross terms. Explains "all 48 orders give the same code".

- **c_str shapes** (RST2): `s = ""; if (p) s = p->data;` and `p ? p->data : ""` give the two retail forms.

- **Batch b76 (main, 007a1870 MATCHED)**: x87 operand order is decided by how each operand is *spelled as an address expression*, and a choice inside an inlined helper can flip an operand pair several statements later. The cross product needed raw float pointers (`pa[n]`, `pb[n]`) for 4 terms, and for `b.X` neither `pb[0]` (two terms wrong) nor the member `b.X` (fixes the cross but flips the later `destination += pos` X pair) worked — `*(&b.Y - 1)` fixed both. When one spelling fixes A and breaks B, try other equivalent address forms of the same operand (`*(&m.Next - 1)`, `(&s)->m`, `*p`, `p[0]`) before changing anything else.
- **Batch b77 (Fable easy-park sweep)**: a converting CIntelligentPointer ctor taking `CThing* const& item` (not by value) reads the pointer through the reference AFTER the base ctor call instead of hoisting it into edi (0096f180); a `__forceinline float Distance(a, b)` helper placed FIRST, then the member stores, lets the scheduler hoist the ParentThing load and `xor edi,edi` above the x87 chain and sink the stores as retail (008dbb30; stores first is worse). A : B))`; literal address forms (`&"A"[0]`, `(c ? A : B) + 0`) and casts do nothing. The `/Os` `push ecx`-before-`fsubp` case is NOT operand order: unary plus, `+0.0f` and address re-spellings of the operands all leave it.

An inline helper taking the pointer or a whole-call ternary does NOT do this.
- **Open family gap: rough-area LevelInfo SIB order (chunks 307/308, ~17 rows)**: retail encodes the per-row Width load as `[hoisted+LevelInfo+4]` (LevelInfo = index) unless the hoisted level*20 is in ebp; ours always makes LevelInfo the base. No change from `level[LevelInfo]`, const& info, CellIndex inline member, `x + Width*y`, integer address arithmetic, vector operator[], unsigned char level, or the QFE compiler. Rows whose hoisted value lands in ebp already match (0079bf30). A user-declared offset variable DOES flip the order (user var = base) but perturbs the whole loop (007fcef0 f2/h1, 142 lines).
- Dead-variable tricks (`f(param++)`, `f(param--)`, `(p = p)`) are folded away; they never change slot packing.
- Chunk 306: passing a literal as an implicit `const CCharString&` temp (or `{}`-scoping a local) lets VC7.1 reuse dead argument slots (007a09b0, 007c6aa0); `GetInterface(&p, n)` out-param form fixed the register order in a `HasTC && field <= 0` test; HasTC returning `unsigned long` gives retail's test forms; `#pragma pack(1)` models the unaligned PItemRemoved at +0x11 (007a09b0).
- **SOLVED -- rough-area LevelInfo SIB order (007fcef0, main, 2026-10-23)**: VC7.1 ranks a *user variable* as the SIB base over a reloaded member pointer, while its own loop-hoisted temps rank below it. Retail's per-level offsets were user variables computed after the y-loop guard: `long y = box.Y0; if (y < box.Y1) { long offset = (unsigned char)level * (long)sizeof(CMapwhoLevelInfo); long cellsOffset = (unsigned char)level * (long)sizeof(CCellVector); for (; y < box.Y1; ++y) ... }` with the cell pointer `&(*(const CCellVector*)((unsigned long)Cells + cellsOffset))._M_start[y * *(const long*)((unsigned long)offset + (unsigned long)LevelInfo + 4) + x]`. Both variables are needed (offset alone fixes Width but flips Cells), in that declaration order (cellsOffset first fails), and declaring them before the guard or with `y` in the for-init perturbs the loop's registers. Seven rounds of expression spellings (subscript swaps, integer sums, comma, sizeof, displacement order) all canonicalise and never move it.
- Chunk 308 (29 landed): the SIB-variable recipe (user variable assigned after the loop guard, one per indexed access) closed 12 FindThingsInRoughArea rows; still open is the loop-exit spill-reload order (009871c0, 00518130, 0063f4b0). Other wins: LP64 tests need `sizeof(long)`-aware checks; a named type-test mask local; list `insert(end(), x)`; Bind filters by reference; cross product by value; GetLengthSquared as `l = Z*Z; l += Y*Y; l += X*X`.
- Chunk 307 (13 landed): `x *= h; x *= h;` gives retail's x87 order for a squared factor; a vfptr plus an `__int64` member pads the vfptr to 8 (put vtable + pad in a base class); 0x49D150 takes a float time (miscounting shifts every frame slot); size temporary primitives from retail's `lea` offset (CEnginePrimitiveSprite = 0x48). Still open: the non-virtual TC call form and the `and byte [mem],imm` RMW form.
- Lander RELOC_IDENTITY on const/non-const overload pairs (GetPlayerManager 0x4C79F0/0x4C7A00, GetMainPlayer 0x449960/0x449970, GetCharacter 0x487DD0/0x487DC0): the const-copy call site must start from `static_cast<const T*>(this)` so the whole chain picks the const overloads (0082db00).
- SIB pool sweep (b79, 3 landed): declaration order alone fixed 009c3ae0 (`minRead` before `size`); a `const C3DMesh2& m = mesh;` alias plus binding the element ref at the loop top fixed 00a9dfa0; on 007c7a00 an explicit `start` variable put the strength-reduced offset FIRST (`lea ecx,[ebx+eax]`), opposite of the user-variable ranking — so the ranking is a tie-break to grid-search, not a fixed rule. Still parked: 007c6460 (16 lines, i ebp vs ebx), 006d8300, 0047042a, 009e5c90/00a39010 (`buffer[m_size]=0` SIB, 30+ placements), 006d4e60, 00b482a0.
- Explicit variable at the SECOND access of a pair (b80, sweep): `CThingAICreature* c2 = Creature.Get();` before the second test gives retail`s `lea ecx,[eax+off]` where reusing one variable gives `add eax ; mov ecx,eax` (00730070); same on 005c71aa (`const long* values = slots->_M_start;` inside the arm, used only at the second access: 88 -> 7 lines). Sharing or hoisting the variable always made it worse. Inline `operator=` as `CThing* item = other.GetPItem(); SetPItem(item);` moves the `lea` after the temp GetPItem.
- Strength-reduced pointer as a user variable (b81, 00a37060 GetClassNumberFromName): walk `const CCharString* n = &first->Name;` advancing `n = &info[1].Name` and recover the entry with offsetof; VC then loads the vector start straight into esi with `add esi,10h` like retail. Second-access locals and entry refs left 3 lines. The rough-area node-loop exit reload order (006fe7c0 and the hand308 twins) resisted 24 box-field orders, map aliases and 4 loop shapes.
- The Linux lane can run /GL + /LTCG (Wine, RTM toolset): `cl /c /O2 /Oy /GL`, then `link /DLL /NOENTRY /LTCG /FORCE:UNRESOLVED /OPT:NOREF /MAP`, then objdump at the map address. With no /EXPORT, LTCG invents custom register conventions (args in edi/eax); export the function and its same-TU callees to keep thiscall. This is a possible route for the `ltcg` gap class in FABLE_LANDING_QUEUE (ecx kept across a call, object in esi), but the grader only reads .obj text, so it would need a link-and-extract mode.
- (b82) A named float intermediate for a call result used inside a larger float expression (`float edge = (float)bank.GetGraphicWidth(...)`) fixed 00645ce9 DrawStatBox. Open family: the /Os float-slot `push ecx` lands before the x `fsubp` in ours, after it in retail (0047e651, 0047f30f, 0047d296); 15 expression/ctor/alias forms did not move it.
- **`ltcg` rows are often same-TU rows (b83, 005c150b):** retail's "ecx left over from the previous call" needed no /GL. A same-TU `__declspec(noinline)` body of the first callee (its landed source) lets VC7.1 see that it preserves ecx, and `#pragma optimize("s", on)` gave the `push [esi]` / `xor eax,eax ; inc eax` shape. Before parking a row as LTCG-only, try the callee's landed body in the same TU.
- `ltcg_grade.py` (lane_tools) grades a candidate under real /GL + /LTCG: it links a DLL with /FORCE:UNRESOLVED, exports the target (and `--export REGEX` callees) to keep thiscall, extracts the function at its map address, and masks .reloc DIR32 fixups and outgoing rel32 calls. It agrees with the ordinary grader on landed rows. Under LTCG, an internal callee gets a custom register convention; an exported one keeps thiscall but its body is no longer used for register knowledge. Taking the callee's address (`g_keep = &C::F;`) keeps both thiscall and the knowledge.
- (b84, 00471865/0080c590) Rows tagged "LTCG convention" are often plain `__fastcall` callees returning a class with a user ctor via a hidden-return pointer in ecx, plus by-value tag temporaries in dead slots; decode the call site from retail and declare them in source. They need no /GL.
- (b84) `ltcg_grade.py` leaf is a substring match; pass a mangled prefix (`'?Copy@CCachedFile'`) when helpers share the name. A target that itself receives register args (00c9cb40, 00c1c3f0, 00c1c330, 00c8a4b0) is not gradable, because the target is always exported, which forces the standard convention.
- (b84) 00c17400 matches only under ltcg_grade (`/EHsc`, same-TU 0xC16CE0 body taking `extra` in esi, address-taken noinline `CWaypointHolder::Get`); it is not landable by the .obj lander.
- (volatile policy, 2026-10-23) `volatile` is allowed only where the original source plausibly had it. Confirmed so far: the SDK `Interlocked*(LONG volatile*)` prototypes (DDK winbase.h), the DirectShow `CUnknown::m_cRef` (combase.h `volatile LONG m_cRef`), and the CFileInstaller cross-thread fields. Hack uses are removed or reworked. 35 landed files lost a hack `volatile` (casts, `volatile char flag` locals, member qualifiers) and still match. The PDB cv-qualifier check is pending on the Windows box: the egor dumps strip cv-qualifiers.
- (chunk 309) `return n > 0 ? true : false;` (or a named bool) avoids the extra `xor eax,eax` before setcc. Write bool WAIT conditions as `!= false` to get `setne`.
- (chunk 309) x87 compares: `<` emits `test ah,5` and `<=` emits `test ah,0x41`. An epsilon retail loads from memory must be an extern variable, not a literal.
- (chunk 309) An implicit wchar_t* to CWideString temporary is built before the pushes; a named local gives `lea`. An __int64 after the vptr needs `#pragma pack(push,4)`.
- (chunk 309) Use xref.py callers to split byte-identical propagated copies across classes (0xE44A40 Bordello vs 0xE53670 BeardyBaldy).
- (chunk 309) VC7.1 tests: a function-pointer typedef that returns a template UDT fails to compile; return void* instead.
- (chunk 310) A same-TU `#pragma auto_inline(off)` body for Compare_00411570 or GetDef 0xCDE280 keeps values in callee-saved registers across the call, as retail does. `__forceinline` CCharString::operator== and the handle operator= when they are used many times.
- (chunk 310) Script loops: `for(;;)` versus `while(!IsActiveThreadTerminating())` decides which cleanup copy jumps and which falls through; put post-loop statements inside the exit branch. `inline_depth(0)` around closing braces and the function-end `return;` places the dtor calls.
- (chunk 310) A by-value CCharString built from a virtual's return needs a declared copy ctor, or it is bit-copied instead of constructed in place. The `// Leaf:` line must hold only the symbol (finish.py); notes go on a `// Label:` line.
- (volatile rework, 2026-10-23) 26 rows that "needed" volatile were reworked without it:
  - IVRefCount re-read (`add eax,4; dec [eax]; cmp [eax],0`): put the count in a second base class at +4 with inline DecRef/GetRefCount and a ReleaseTemporary helper (00537956, 005bd8a0, 005bde50; model 005ee207).
  - Re-read argument slot: the value is really `float`; VC7.1 never holds a float in an integer register across branches (0040b310, 00891220).
  - "Volatile float global": 0x0123822C/0x0122DC88 are the compiler literals 24.0f and 1/24.
  - Component lookups: GetTCOut / GetTCIfPresent out-parameter forms.
  - Hand-built vtable stores: real polymorphic classes with inline-zeroed members.

  Only 009e1da0 (GFAXToVX, `fld qword g; fmul dword x` operand order) still needs volatile; it stays landed with a FIDELITY NOTE header (owner, 2026-10-23) until the PDB is checked.
- (chunk 311) ~CWorldMap 0x506940 matched only with three changes together: an inline (first, last) region-destroy helper, an SAllocTag with a user ctor, and a conditional free of the map header plus a Release that nulls the pointer unconditionally. Most other CWorldMap rows show whole-program register caching across calls; park them early as the lane gap.
- (chunk 311) C2DBoxI needs a user copy ctor. Write an int-to-float expression out again at each use rather than sharing it. Switch case order in the source shapes the emitted layout. Bind2/IsNot filter temporaries sit in dead argument slots. 0xBFEA0E is the malloc thunk.
- (R&D pair, b85) Frame/slot diff where a local's address goes to a declaration-only callee: first try a same-TU `#pragma auto_inline(off)` body for that callee (address-taking ctors/dtors count too). This gave 5 of 7 matches (004fde80, 0063d960, 0063db30, 0056be00, 0087cb10). Drop a stand-in that makes the frame smaller than retail.
- (R&D) Slot order (VC7.1 /O2, esp frame): scalars of 4 bytes or less come first, then aggregates and temporaries. Within each group the order is static live reference count descending, then first use. Declaration order, names and loops are inert, and sibling `{}` scopes overlay. Dead references (`(void)x`, `if(0)`, unused pointer, `x=x`) do not count.
- (R&D) Leave tag/comparator parameters (random_access_iterator_tag, __true_type) unnamed in a visible non-inlined body (004b3a20). `(void)x;` right after a float store can flip one x87 commutative pair (00751610), but a family sweep gave 0/43, so use it only for an exact single-pair diff.
- (R&D) Only the `const long*` LowerBound 0x0040F020 responds to stand-ins; the `const long&` 0x004365B0 key keeps its own slot. TU noise, identifier names and /GL+/LTCG never move a function's codegen. Some parked bases don't reproduce their recorded size; re-grade the base first.
- (b85) Tests must print `<addr>_TEST PASS` and must not use dynamic_cast: the lander compiles tests /GR-, so use a virtual type query instead.
- (R&D E-10, 0072c790) Before slot-sharing work, sizeof-check every by-value local and temporary type against the PDB (`struct_layouts_egor.tsv`). A parked model with a 12-byte CBaseIntelligentPointer (the PDB says 8: vtable + PData) gave frame 0x130 v 0x128; fixing the size matched.
- (R&D E-9, 008d0fb0) Two inline helpers that each declare their own out-variable get separate frame slots. Retail used ONE function-scope out-variable shared by both arms, plus same-TU GetEvent bodies.
- (sweep b87) Sweeping I-3 (same-TU noinline bodies for address-taking callees) across older parked rows closes rows: 5 in the first batch. The SIZE check against the PDB matched 0 of 1499 rows, because PDB sizes often differ from retail (00725b70's functor is 20 bytes in retail, 24 in the PDB). Trust the retail frame over the PDB size.
- (R&D E-16/E-17, b88) Drop member-alias locals: replace `CMapVector* maps = &TempMaps;` or `T* header = Parent->Map->Header;` by the expression at each use. The alias's own register web can steal rank from `this` or a value retail keeps live.
  - Closed 00518130 (the loop-exit reload family, previously listed as a gap), 00bac0f0 (spill-slot swap) and 00be79d0.
  - It is narrow: 3 gains in 213 rows. Apply it before statement reordering, since field-store order only shows once the alias web is gone.
- (R&D R-15) Spill slots and address-taken locals share ONE ranking by reference count (a `long x; take(&x)` with 2 refs ranks after the spills; with 4 refs it takes the first slot).
- (R&D E-18) The member-alias drop does NOT fix the rough-area NODE-loop exit reload order. Retail reloads the box-bound spill (edx) first, then map (edi) and box.Y1 (esi); ours emits edi, esi, edx. About 20 FindThingsInRoughArea rows were tried, all inert. That web still looks like a lane gap; 00518130 was the different level-loop exit web.
- (sweep b89) VC7.1 reads stand-in bodies. A stand-in that writes only one word lets the caller drop stores that the body never reads (for example a vector's third word). A stand-in that touches every byte of its object closed 5 rows the write-only stand-ins missed, especially for DTORs (00d08790: a ~CTimer body alone took it from 766v730 to a match).
- (R&D E-19, b90) x87 commutative operand order can be re-keyed by k unused named locals at function entry. They emit no bytes, and the effect is periodic in the symbol count (period 8). 00b81050 matched with k=1 or 2 and 00b990b0 with k chosen the same way. Treat this as a last resort for a pure single-pair x87 residue, and note it in the `// Grade:` header (it is a symbol-table artefact, not proof of the original source).
- (chunk 312) GFFloatRandom 0x4978A0 is `__fastcall(float range, unsigned long* seed)`, so the seed call comes before the range push. Separate `Terminate(); return;` exits merge as retail does; an `||` chain does not. Writing the final branch inverted (failure arm first) picks retail's cross-jump tail.
- (chunk 312) Give each call its own scoped `bool reached`; VC7.1 then packs them into a dead argument slot. nd.py hides `sub esp` differences, so check frame size with udiff (action object sizes such as ProjectileCrouch 0x124 are the usual cause).
- (chunk 312) Yield in the 1–2 KB band is low: 6 of 40, with 19 skipped (inline-asm fistp rounding, x87, budget). Ten large rows were only decoded and stay in the pool.
- (sweep b91) CBaseIntelligentPointer is 8 bytes in retail (vtable + PData). Parked models written as `{vftable; m_pItem; m_link}` plus a derived `virtual v00` come out at 16 bytes. Fixing the size alone did not match; the frame closed only together with an I-3 stand-in (00722590, 007c25a0). A const& callee such as push_back(const T&) needs a stand-in body that READS the value. There is no shared header for it: about 1,979 landed files carry a local model.
- (sweep b92) GetPBestThing / GetPBestThingInRoughArea stand-in: a same-TU noinline body that loops over candidates, reads every bound filter and the compare position, and writes *out. It closed 4 AIStateGroup finders (0090c1a0, 00964ca0, 00937b10, 00937c20). A vector::resize stand-in must fill new elements with a byte-by-byte copy of x; a member-wise copy or memcpy shrinks the caller (005dd440).
- (R&D E-26, b93) Dead-argument-slot packing (00583a92) took three changes together: a branch-scoped `bool used` out-flag, a `__forceinline` ~CDefPointer, and an STLport `*(begin()+n)` operator[]. With them, VC7.1 packs the locals into the dead quickSlot argument slot.
- (R&D, b93) 00aee240 and 00b393d0 matched by reusing the landed SetInverse text inline plus k unused locals (the x87 re-key lever, E-19). Copy the landed callee text verbatim when retail inlines a landed helper.
- (sweep final) The lever sweep closed 21 parked rows: 19 with I-3 alone, 2 with SIZE+I-3. I-6, I-7, SIZE alone and the LowerBound(const long*) body (0 of 258) close nothing on their own. A "search tools" stand-in body is reusable: a loop over candidates, extern filter predicates, and the Nearest compare's position read in place (generator `$S/w22/rnd/tools/gensearch.py`).
- (sweep final) gq.sh's diff count masks branch targets and global operands. A "0 diff" DIFFER row can still have wrong control flow: 007534b0 falls through where retail returns false. Read the full disassembly before calling a row "close".
- (R&D final, rounds 1–2, all 9 families) 19 R&D matches plus 21 sweep matches. Per-family rules are in `$S/w22/rnd/FAMILY_NOTES.md`. One-liners:
  - x87 commutative mem/mem operand order is keyed on the function's symbol count mod 8. Try k = 0..7 unused named locals before the operands' first use. Re-sweep k after any shape change. It moves operand order only, never registers, slots or scheduling.
  - The engine 3x3 inverse is the landed 00aee560 `CMatrix3x4::SetInverse` text; paste it verbatim through implicit `this` rather than respelling the cofactors.
  - An address-taken temporary (a condition's conversion temp, a `bool&` out-flag, a `const long&` key) holds its frame or dead-arg slot for its whole LEXICAL scope. Close it with a `__forceinline` condition helper, a `{}` block, or a per-branch declaration (00583a92, 0064fc23). Plain locals use liveness, so wrapping them in blocks is inert.
  - An early-return chain keeps the LAST merged copy of `return K`; a result variable with a single return keeps the first.
  - A lost tail call (`jmp [eax+N]` becoming `call` plus pops) means an upstream CSE pulled in a callee-saved register. An STLport `*(begin()+n)` operator[] stops VC materialising `&v[i]` as a CSE; `_M_start[n]` does not.
  - Dtor inline budget: a `__forceinline` dtor plus `#pragma inline_depth(0)` regions inside the body gave partials (00e2eac0, 00ce1280).
  - Decide a match from the q.py status line only; diff-line counts hide reorders among relocation lines.
- (near-miss b95) A filter-chain pointer store scheduled after a ctor call is fixed by a full-object reader body for the consuming FindMostRecentMessage. A Bind3 chain also needs operator&& to build the Bind3 from the bound operands (00934920, 0091cdc0).
- (near-miss b95) A component lookup whose miss path reloads a pointer retail keeps in a register: return the interface by value (null by default) from a forceinline helper instead of through an out-parameter (008c9fe0).
- (near-miss b95) Callees returning a CDefPointer by value respond to I-3 stand-ins only with a DEFINED add-ref copy ctor (005eede2). Read FramesPerSecond into a local ahead of the call when retail spills it first. The k-lever also flips fiadd versus fild/faddp.
- (R&D R-36, b96) x87 commutative pair re-keyed through a reference: `const C3DVector& cam = DragCamPos;` makes the operand a pointer-register read (`fsub [ecx+4]`). Naming the 2D length (`float lengthSq = offset.GetLengthSquared2D();`) fixed the `Y*s` pairs; both together matched 0075cad0. Coupled pairs (00547050) can need a BY-VALUE `operator*=(C2DVector)` plus the alias.
- (R&D round 3, I-29 measured) VC7.1 hands out callee-saved registers by WEIGHTED REAL reference count. Uses in loops weigh more; uses after an early return or in one if/else arm weigh less. Ties go to the earlier definition. Live-range span, dead references, copies, condition spelling (`||` versus separate ifs) and block shape do not count. To move a value into a different register, change how many real (weighted) uses it has, or reorder definitions on a tie.
- (R&D round 3) x87 commutative operand order is keyed per field value by the base symbol it is first read through. A `const T& alias = obj;` or an inline member (`GetLengthSquared2D()`) flips every product on that value, which the symbol-count k cannot do. Mixing alias and direct reads of one field does nothing (value numbering merges them, first spelling wins). `operator=` written as `CThing* item = other.GetPItem(); SetPItem(item);` moves a `lea ebp` after GetPItem (0091c3d0).
- (R&D round 3) /GS does not reorder non-array locals in VC7.1. Objects with dtors and equal refs take reverse declaration order. VC7.1 deletes a dead zero-initialised local even when its address is taken, so a never-read zero dword in retail is something else. Identifier names never matter.
- (chunk 315) A user `__forceinline` dtor plus `inline_depth(0)` around returns gives inline copies at plain returns and the out-of-line copy at the pragma returns and closing brace. Put the terminate check at the top of `for(;;)` (00e02e50).
- (chunk 315) CCountedPointer::operator= must read the source pair before the self-compare, and both it and CScriptThing's operator= need `__forceinline` (00e4e0e0). An inline `operator->` on a def pointer gives `mov eax / lea ecx`; `.m_pDef->` gives `add ecx`.
- (chunk 315) Under lane flags wchar_t is not native: declare `CWideString(const unsigned short*)`. `$S/w17/xdis.py` takes its length in HEX.
- (chunk 315) 19 of its 28 parks are one-grade placeholders (x87-heavy over 1 KB, or decode-only budget parks), not real attempts. Treat them as unworked in a retry.
- (chunk 313) Packed classes need `#pragma pack(1)`. A member-function-pointer table needs `#pragma pointers_to_members(full_generality, multiple_inheritance)`; the `__multiple_inheritance` keyword fails with C2292. `(begin() + i)->` stops strength reduction. `(a - b).LengthSquared()` then sqrt gives the Z,Y,X sum order.
- (chunk 313) Three register-only rows (009647c0, 009658b0, 00927f30) resisted the by-value FindInterface, weighted-count and scope levers; they are queued for R&D.
- (2026-10-23) Chunk 314 (8/40): `#pragma inline_depth(0)` around ONE return or throw moves only that exit's dtor/ctor out of line, which is the dtor-inline-budget split (009a8840 47→17, 009d0880 47→42 blocks, still parked). `(float)fabs(x)` gives retail's dword compare where plain fabs compares a double. An iterator class with a user copy ctor is built in its stack-argument slot. An incremental `c = …; c += …;` dot product keeps the Y,X,Z load order. The STLport map dtor must be `__forceinline` in big functions, and retail maps are 12 bytes in frames. AngleDifference 0x00A441E0 is `__stdcall`. In CCreatureActionBase, retail CutSceneEvents is at +0x90, not +0x94.
- (2026-10-23, R&D I-35, 004091c0) /GS push_back tag bytes packed into one flag dword: make the vector's `_M_insert_overflow` a same-TU `template <class Tag> __declspec(noinline)` member template with the tag parameter UNNAMED. The empty tag temporaries then share retail's `more` dword (frame 0x250).
- (2026-10-23, R&D round 3b) Dtor inlining:
  - A member dtor reached through an implicit or plain-inline enclosing dtor is called out of line even when it is itself `__forceinline`; mark the enclosing dtor `__forceinline` too.
  - `#pragma inline_depth(n)` with n ≥ 1 does not cut a dtor chain at a scope exit. Only depth 0 blocks it, and depth 0 blocks at the outermost dtor.
- (2026-10-23, R&D round 3b) Dead stores and block layout:
  - A dead zero store survives only into memory that escapes, so make it a member of the aggregate whose address escapes (00e2eac0 went from 42 lines to 2).
  - Block layout comes from the CFG after tail merge. Where a label or `return` sits in the source does not choose which merged exit copy survives.
  - Loop shape (while, if+do-while, a bool flag) is canonicalised before the exit head-hoist decision, so the exit head-merge (00da6d50, 00da7830) is still a gap.
- (2026-10-23, R&D round 3b) Register and x87 limits:
  - The R-29 priority rule cannot reach a free ebx/ebp choice or a web-coalescing decision (00846dd0, 009647c0).
  - An x87 per-value operand key survives intervening possibly-aliasing stores: a later read of the same field keeps the first read's key.
- (2026-10-23, R&D round 4)
  - A same-TU noinline stand-in's body is VISIBLE to its callers. If it never reads a by-reference argument, VC7.1 deletes the caller's stores to that local, so stand-ins must read every input.
  - The empty-tag lever generalises past `_M_insert_overflow`: a noinline `find` stand-in with the tag unnamed matched 008e7040, and 008865c0 (dead-parameter slot) matched once its stand-in read `x`.
  - 00ce1280: the loop-2 depth-0 exits `goto` a label on loop 1's depth-0 return, which selects retail's merged exit copy (I-42).
- (2026-10-23, retry of ChatGPT's 960-962 parks, 6/59)
  - **Receiver spelling:** writing the receiver as `typed->HasDriverType(n)` rather than `thing->` re-weights register assignment across the WHOLE function (005185f0, 00518900).
  - **Byte-twins:** a byte-twin's landed body transplants (00746560 and 007f9c00 from 00905d10).
  - **Named float temp:** `float a=(float)alpha; alpha=(long)(a*fade);` gives fild/fmulp instead of fimul.
  - **Getter temporaries:** chained by-value getter temporaries are read through the returned pointer (`fild [eax]`), while a named local reads its stack slot.
  - **Ctor stand-ins:** they must not expose member values; a visible zero shrank the code.
  - **Include paths:** an absolute `/workspace/...` include hangs Wine cl.
  - **Test layout:** tests that `#include` the source need the source inlined before landing.
- (2026-10-23, R&D I-44, closes the "exit head-merge" gap) In script `Main` wait loops, write the IsAlive test through a layout view:
  - the loop is `while (reinterpret_cast<const CScriptThingView&>(thing).Thing().IsAlive())`;
  - with `struct CScriptThingView { void* vptr; void* Data; void* Info; const CScriptThing& Thing() const { return *reinterpret_cast<const CScriptThing*>(this); } };`.

  VC7.1 then hoists `lea ecx,[thing]` above the terminate `jne` into the shared `~CScriptThing` exit, as retail does. Matched 00da6d50, 00da7830, 00e11450 and 00d6aea0. A named view reference and an `AsThing(const void*)` helper are inert. Fidelity caveat: the view is a compiler lever, probably not the original spelling.
- (2026-10-23, R&D R-47) I-44 sweep:

- (2026-10-23, R&D round 4 wrap-up)
  - **I-44 needs a value local and an unmerged reference.** VC7.1 folds a CScriptThing reference back to the object and devirtualises it, so the view cast forms `this`. The view cast works only on a VALUE local.
  - **Sibling scan.** Retail functions that byte-match a landed body apart from call targets compile from the landed source. Scan for them with call targets normalised (`$S/w22/rnd/r4/`) before re-authoring. BUT a verbatim port binds the same mangled callee to a second retail address: put the port's model in its own namespace (004f97b3 uses `NPairLongMapTree`).
  - **The lander does not catch this.** It only WARNS on a cross-row `RELOC_CONFLICT`, and the duplicate binding is silently not recorded.
  - **Unchangeable order.** `this`-before-arguments order for a global interface pointer cannot be moved from source (00d910b0).
  - **Dtor arm stays inline.** I-43 (goto to an existing exit) leaves 00d05a60/00d06130 at 1400v1400, because retail puts the CPauseNonScripted dtor arm cold after `ret`.
- (2026-10-23) Chunk 316 (render/engine ctors, 15/40):
  - **Shader lookups:** a named resource local read once before the first lookup gives retail's edi-cached run; an inline getter reloads the global every time.
  - **Fill loops:** VC7.1 fully unrolls a 4-iteration colour-fill loop, so write the loop.
  - **Loop fall-through:** when retail falls through from a loop, leave that branch's `return` out.
  - **Generated manager dtors:** declare no dtor on the manager, and give the shader wrappers a `__forceinline virtual` dtor.
  - **SetState chains:** a named mask local moves where the test/or operands swap.

These are the long-standing catalog backlog (`rebuild/oracles/reloc_audit.tsv`), mostly byte-twin callees that share one name: LowerBound 0x40F020/0x4365B0, GetMainPlayer 0x449960/0x449970, the CBaseClass dtors 0x99A2E0/0x99A300, the CBaseIntelligentPointer ctor/dtor pairs, and g_MapwhoLevels +0/+4.

- (2026-10-23) Chunk 317 (engine renderers, quest-script mains, 13/40):
  - **Flag from an `&&` chain:** write `bool found = a() && b(); if (found)`. An if/else assigning true/false misplaces the false store.
  - **Same method inlined and called:** CScriptThing::IsAlive is expanded inline once and called once in the same function. Model the inline site with a `__forceinline` helper.
  - **Float constant:** 0.5f at 0x0122F59C is a literal. Declaring it extern flips the x87 operand order.
  - **Global read order:** a global read into a named local fixes the x87 load order.
  - **Index/stream calls:** SetIndices/SetStreamSource are written as if/else whole calls.
  - **Header bug:** retail CEngineWaterRenderer arrays are 12 bytes, not the 16 in engine/CEngineWaterRenderer.h.
  - **VC7.1 tests:** `T t = T();` does not zero-initialise; use memset.
  - **Script vtable:** CScriptThing slot 21 is MsgIsHitBy and slot 75 is IsAlive.
  - **Open gap:** pure SetState test/or operand swaps; park them early.
- (2026-10-23) Follow-up on ChatGPT's 14 large 960-962 rows (1/14; 008df680 NCreatureMode::CModeThunderLightningStorm::OnDeactivate):
  - **erase:** an STLport erase written with inline `CopyPtrs(last,_Last,first)` / `Destroy(i,_Last)` helpers fixes a last/out register swap.
  - **GetTC:** retail GetTC often inlines the lower_bound len/half loop instead of calling LowerBound.
  - **Scope sharing:** a local declared after an if-block does not share that block's slots until it is wrapped in `{}` (007a6760 frame 0x98→0x88).
  - **Empty tags:** a user ctor on an empty tag removes its byte store.
  - **Colour structs:** byte stores in the ctor body (not an init list) give retail's store order.

When retail calls the MSVCR71 array thunks (`operator new[]` 0x00BFEB22, `operator delete[]` 0x00BFEB1C), declare both.
